### [1] [PragyaDoc: A Universal Document Intelligence Framework for Multilingual Medical Document Understanding in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07478)
*Jagpal Singh Jhala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PragyaDoc，一个通用文档智能框架，旨在解决印度22种官方语言背景下医疗文档仅以英文编写导致的可及性障碍。该系统通过四层流水线实现医疗文档的提取、结构化和本地化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度拥有22种官方语言，但绝大多数医疗文档仅以英文编写，导致农村人口、ASHA工作者和患者家属等最需要这些信息的人群无法理解，形成严重的医疗信息可及性壁垒。

Method: PragyaDoc采用四层流水线架构：(1) 并行集成OCR提取层，负责文档文字识别；(2) 几何-词汇融合层，结合版面几何信息与词汇特征；(3) 确定性领域结构化层，遵循规则进行医学文档结构化；(4) 双LLM医学推理与本地化层，通过两个大语言模型协同完成医学推理和语言本地化翻译。

Result: 论文尚未在摘要中展示具体实验结果，但框架的设计目标是通过四层流水线实现医疗文档从英文到多种印度本土语言的高质量转换与本地化。

Conclusion: PragyaDoc作为一个通用文档智能框架，为印度多语言环境下的医疗文档可及性问题提供了系统化的解决方案，有望弥合医疗服务提供者与基层患者群体之间的语言和信息鸿沟。

Abstract: India's 22 official languages create a critical accessibility barrier: the majority of medical documentation exists exclusively in English, yet the patients who most urgently require this information - rural populations, ASHA workers, and patient families - are functionally excluded from understanding it. This paper presents PragyaDoc, a Universal Document Intelligence Framework that addresses this gap through a four-layer pipeline: a parallel ensemble OCR extraction layer, a geometric-lexical fusion layer, a deterministic domain structuring layer, and a dual-LLM medical reasoning and localization layer

</details>


### [2] [NeuroPilot: An Agent-Driven Smart Pipeline for Processing, Quality Control, and Managing Neuroimages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07541)
*Yiyao Chen,Yucheng Li,Jungong Tong,Shaoqi Wang,Kunhao Zhou,Ziquan Wei,Monica Murea,Marissa DiPiero,Tingting Dan,Guorong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroPilot是一个多智能体系统，将神经影像数据标准化、预处理和质量控制三个环节数字化为三个LLM可调用的技能模块，实现全自动工作流编排。该系统已在17个队列（超过12.3万被试）中部署验证，涵盖婴儿到老龄人群及多种MRI模态，将传统2-3个月的处理周期压缩至一周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经影像数据处理依赖三个脆弱环节：数据标准化、模态特定预处理和质量控制。虽然单个工具成熟，但其编排需要项目特定脚本、环境适配调优和大量人工QC，耗时费力且可扩展性差。

Method: NeuroPilot采用多智能体架构，将三类专业知识数字化为LLM可调用的技能：dcm2bids-skill（数据标准化）、neuroimage-pre-skill（模态特定预处理）和qc-agent-skill（质量控制）。LLM驱动的智能体自动编排工作流，通过统一配置适应不同基础设施。QC采用基于3D浏览器仪表盘的半自动多层级验证系统，异常案例自动上报主管审查。

Result: 在17个队列（12.3万+被试）中验证，覆盖婴儿到老龄人群及结构、弥散、功能多种MRI模态。QC智能体筛查了558例生产被试，验证自动标记与FreeSurfer拓扑缺陷指标的一致性。婴儿处理流程在QC验证输入上实现100%（201/201）完成率。处理周期从2-3个月压缩至一周。

Conclusion: NeuroPilot通过LLM驱动的多智能体系统成功实现了神经影像数据处理全流程的自动化编排，在广泛人群和模态中展现出良好通用性与可扩展性，显著提升了处理效率，为大规模神经影像数据中心化处理提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Transforming raw neuroimage archives into analysis-ready derivatives relies on three brittle stages: data standardization, modality-specific preprocessing, and quality control (QC). While individual neuroimaging tools are well developed, their orchestration requires project-specific scripts, environment-adaptive tuning, and labor-intensive manual QC. To address this, we introduce NeuroPilot, a multi-agent system that digitalizes the expertise of neuroimage processing, QC, and data management into three LLM-invocable skills: dcm2bids-skill, neuroimage-pre-skill, and qc-agent-skill. The LLM-driven agent autonomously orchestrates workflows, generalizing various infrastructure settings into a single configuration to achieve the highest scalability. Demonstrating the system's generalizability, we deployed NeuroPilot across 17 cohorts (>123,000 subjects) spanning infant to aging populations and multiple MRI modalities (structural, diffusion, functional). In practice, after standardizing data via the dcm2bids-skill, the agent dynamically routes datasets to the optimal neuroimage-pre-skill based on available modalities and cohort traits (e.g., dispatching T1w and fMRI data to fMRIPrep, or selecting specialized pipelines for infant cohorts). The qc-agent-skill then drives an evidence-based, semi-automated QC via a 3-D browser dashboard, utilizing a multi-tiered verification system to optimize failed cases and escalate complex issues for supervisor inspection. Quantitatively, our QC agent screened 558 production subjects, validating its automated flags against FreeSurfer's topology-defect metrics. The infant processing pipeline achieved a 100% (201/201) completion rate on QC-validated inputs. Importantly, NeuroPilot compresses the traditional 2--3 month timeline for training staff and processing complete datasets into a single week. NeuroPilot is deployed in https://wanda-cyberbench.com/.

</details>


### [3] [Performance of large language models in the optical diagnosis of colorectal polyps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07543)
*Joshua C. Vences,William T. Tran,Nikko Gimpaya,Catharine M. Walsh,Rishad J. Khan,Robert Bechara,Asher C. Wiggins,Celine N. Rousan,Kaitlyn V. G. L. Morgado,Angie Ibrahim,Kevin H. M. Kuo,Daniel von Renteln,Alexander Hann,Dennis L. Shung,Michael A. Scaffidi,Charles Ménard,Joshua Landy,Samir C. Grover*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五个多模态大语言模型（Claude Opus 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-o3、GPT-4o、GPT-5）在结直肠息肉光学诊断中的准确性，基于PRIME数据集的132例病例，比较其在Paris分类、NICE分类和组织学预测方面的表现。结果显示所有模型在区分肿瘤性vs非肿瘤性息肉时F1>0.9，Gemini 2.5 Pro在浸润性vs非浸润性息肉及低vs高级别腺瘤方面表现最佳，但敏感性和特异性均未达到ESGE标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠息肉的准确光学诊断对指导切除策略和随访至关重要，多模态大语言模型展现出用于图像诊断的潜力，但目前缺乏对其诊断准确性的系统性评估，需要验证MLLM在临床部署前的可靠性。

Method: 采用PRIME数据集的132例白光和窄带成像（NBI）图像进行回顾性诊断性能研究，评估五个MLLM（Claude Opus 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-o3、GPT-4o、GPT-5）在Paris分类、NICE分类及组织学预测中的表现，计算F1分数、正确率百分比和准确率，并与专家响应对比，使用Cochran's Q和McNemar检验确定模型间差异。

Result: 所有MLLM在肿瘤性vs非肿瘤性息肉中的F1分数均>0.9；Gemini 2.5 Pro在浸润性vs非浸润性息肉（F1=0.560）和低vs高级别腺瘤（F1=0.492）中F1分数最高；Claude Opus 4和GPT-5在Paris分类中正确率最高（41.7%），具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: Claude Opus 4和Gemini 2.5 Pro在区分息肉亚型方面准确性最高，最接近专家共识，但敏感性和特异性未达到ESGE标准，需要前瞻性多中心试验和设计人机协作（human-in-the-loop）工作流程后才能考虑临床部署。

Abstract: Background and Study Aims: Accurate optical diagnosis of colorectal polyps guides resection strategy and surveillance, with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) showing potential for image-based diagnosis. We aimed to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of MLLMs in classifying colorectal polyps and predicting histology. Methods: We conducted a retrospective diagnostic performance study using the PRIME dataset, a curated set of white light and narrow-band imaging (NBI) images. We evaluated Claude Opus 4, Google Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT-o3, GPT-4o, and GPT-5. For Paris, Narrow-band Imaging Colorectal Endoscopic (NICE), and predicted histology, we calculated F1 scores, percent correct scores, and accuracy of each MLLM compared to expert responses for 132 cases. Cochran's Q and McNemar's Test were used to determine differences between predicted values of each MLLM. Results: The F1 scores among MLLMs were >0.9 for all models for neoplastic vs. non-neoplastic polyps. Gemini 2.5 Pro demonstrated the highest F1 scores for invasive vs. non-invasive polyps and low- vs. high-grade adenoma, at 0.560 and 0.492 respectively. Claude Opus 4 and GPT-5 had statistically significantly higher percent correct scores than other MLLMs at 41.7%, using Paris classification. Conclusions: Claude Opus 4 and Gemini 2.5 Pro showed the highest accuracy in differentiating polyp subtypes, performing closest to expert consensus. Sensitivity and specificity, however, did not meet ESGE standards, highlighting the need for prospective multicenter trials and the design of human-in-the-loop workflows before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [4] [Learning an Interior Layout Policy in a Domain Specific Language Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07547)
*Yuhao Lu,Weichen Zhang,Wenyi Xiao,Haohui Chen,Yiyun Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LayoutDSL，一个基于LLM的室内场景布局生成框架，在DSL（领域特定语言）动作空间中学习布局策略，每个DSL动作对应可解释的设计决策，并通过领域奖励的强化学习进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内布局生成方法存在两方面的根本问题：(1) 将房间条件简化为粗粒度的3D边界框，忽略门窗等结构元素；(2) 将空间推理建模为连续坐标回归，难以学习智能布局设计背后的推理逻辑。

Method: 提出LayoutDSL框架，在领域特定语言（DSL）动作空间中学习室内布局策略。具体包括：(1) DSL提供布局信息的显式符号表示，每个动作对应可解释的设计决策；(2) 构建3D-FrontDSL数据集，包含房间结构标注及合成的DSL动作序列用于监督微调；(3) 设计基于室内设计原则和物理合理性的奖励，并通过强化学习优化策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，LayoutDSL在空间合理性和设计逻辑性方面显著优于强基线和现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过在符号化DSL动作空间中进行策略学习并配合可验证的奖励反馈，能够有效提升室内布局生成的推理逻辑性和可泛化性，为智能室内设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Indoor scene layout generation is a challenging task in interior design. Existing methods often oversimplify the task by reducing room conditions to coarse 3D bounding boxes and neglecting structural elements such as doors and windows. More fundamentally, many prior approaches formulate spatial reasoning as direct coordinate prediction, thereby casting interior layout design as continuous regression over raw geometric parameters, which hinders the model from learning the underlying reasoning logic of intelligent layout design. We propose \textbf{LayoutDSL}, a novel LLM-based framework for learning an interior layout policy in a domain-specific language (DSL) action space. The DSL provides an explicit symbolic representation of layout information and serves as a structured action space for layout reasoning, where each action corresponds to an interpretable design decision. Under this DSL-based policy learning paradigm, we construct 3D-FrontDSL, a dataset of room-structure annotations paired with synthetic DSL action sequences for supervised fine-tuning. To promote a more generalizable and scalable policy with verifiable feedback, we design rewards grounded in interior design principles and physical plausibility, and optimize the policy via reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LayoutDSL substantially improves spatial plausibility and design logicality over strong baselines and existing methods.

</details>


### [5] [P2Voxel: Pyramid Pivot Voxelization for 3D Mesh Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07549)
*Zhenhong Sun,Haozhe Liu,Yifu Wang,Xibin Song,Senbo Wang,Huadong Mo,Daoyi Dong,Hongdong Li,Pan Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P2Voxel，一种金字塔枢轴体素化框架，将三角网格的tokenization重新定义为"局部表面证据采样"，通过局部平面性假设用表面枢轴和方向符号表示每个活动体素（提供最小化且可确定性重建的证据），通过空间复杂度假设对几何复杂区域分配更细粒度token、平滑区域保持粗粒度，并通过块可重建性假设在可局部重建的枢轴块上学习紧凑的多分辨率潜在码。该方法将网格转化为紧凑、结构化、可学习的金字塔枢轴token，实现高效的下游3D网格重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角网格提供精确的表面几何，但其不规则的拓扑连接使3D网格tokenization成为一个几何采样问题。现有方法要么是场中心的体采样，要么是边交点的表面采样，都未能充分利用网格作为局部表面证据的显式特性。作者希望找出一套方案：仅在每个活动体素内采样最少的几何证据，即可确定性地恢复完整表面，从而获得紧凑且可学习的网格token。

Method: P2Voxel基于三个关键创新：(1) 局部平面性假设下的"枢轴体素化"，每个活动体素由一个表面枢轴点和方向符号表示，该最小证据可诱导出确定性重建所需的角点值；(2) 空间复杂度假设下的"金字塔枢轴体素化"，根据真实表面的空间非均匀性，对几何复杂区域分配更细的枢轴token，平滑区域保持粗粒度紧凑表示；(3) 块可重建性假设下的"金字塔VAE"，在可局部重建的枢轴块上学习紧凑的多分辨率潜在码，避免将整个高分辨率体素化形状建模为稠密全局场。

Result: P2Voxel将网格转换为紧凑、结构化、可学习的金字塔枢轴token，使网格重建在下游3D任务中高效可行。三个假设分别保证了局部证据的最小性、空间分配的自适应性以及块级潜在编码的可重建性，共同实现了比场中心和边交点方法更紧凑且保留重建能力的网格表示。

Conclusion: P2Voxel通过重新定义网格tokenization为局部表面证据采样，将显式几何信息以最小化、结构化且可学习的方式编码为金字塔枢轴token，为3D网格重建及相关下游任务提供了一种紧凑且重建感知的tokenization框架。

Abstract: Triangle meshes provide explicit and accurate surface geometry, yet their irregular topology connectivity makes 3D mesh tokenization a geometric sampling problem: how to sample and organize geometric evidence into compact, structured and learnable tokens. Beyond field-centric volumetric sampling and edge-intersection surface sampling, we retarget mesh tokenization as \textit{local surface evidence sampling}: identifying the minimal geometric evidence inside each active voxel that is sufficient for deterministic surface recovery. To this end, we introduce \textbf{P2Voxel}, a pyramid pivot voxelization framework for compact and reconstruction-aware mesh tokenization. P2Voxel is built on three key innovations. Under the \textit{Local Planarity} assumption, Pivot Voxelization represents each active voxel with a surface pivot and an orientation sign, providing minimal local evidence that can induce the corner values required for deterministic reconstruction. Under the \textit{Spatial Complexity} assumption, Pyramid Pivot Voxelization exploits the spatial non-uniformity of real surfaces by allocating finer pivot tokens to geometrically complex regions while keeping smooth regions coarse and compact. Under the \textit{Block Reconstructability} assumption, a Pyramid VAE learns compact multi-resolution latent codes over locally reconstructable pivot blocks, avoiding the need to model the entire high-resolution voxelized shape as a dense global field. Together, these designs convert meshes into compact, structured, and learnable pyramid pivot tokens, enabling efficient mesh reconstruction for downstream 3D tasks.

</details>


### [6] [Auditing Medical Vision-Language Models on Chest Radiographs: Estimating Reference Agreement Across Institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07550)
*Pengyang Yu,Yiou Wang,Zhongping Dong,Sahraoui Dhelim,Chun-Mei Feng,M. Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种生成式视觉语言模型在三个机构的胸部X光语料库、六种发现、两种提示协议下的表现（超过34.5万条发现级预测），研究如何用少量本地标签估算接收机构处的"发现×方向"参考一致性。结果发现自适应选择七个估计器不如固定使用Beta-Binomial经验贝叶斯估计器或仅用目标逻辑回归模型，且跨机构聚合的估计器优势不具稳健性，参考一致性必须按站点和接口重新评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型返回的结构化胸部X光片发现没有置信度评分，接收机构无法判断单个判定可信程度如何；同时，跨站点、跨发现、跨预测方向和跨问题格式的一致性是否成立基本上未被测量，因此需要系统评估并探索用少量本地标签估算参考一致性的方法。

Method: 在三个机构语料库上评估三种生成式视觉语言模型，涉及六种发现和两种提示协议，生成超过34.5万条发现级预测；然后从少量本地标签预算出发，估计"发现×方向"参考一致性。通过反复的机构留出交叉验证对七种估计策略进行压力测试，并比较自适应选择与固定替代方案（Beta-Binomial经验贝叶斯、仅目标逻辑回归、跨机构池化估计等）的表现。

Result: 排除接收机构参与开发时，自适应估计器选择（平均Brier 0.1083）不如固定简单替代方案：Beta-Binomial经验贝叶斯为0.0853，仅目标逻辑回归为0.0855，两者差值0.0003小于求解器版本变化带来的敏感性，且各在约半数设置中占优，无法推荐默认方案。跨机构池化估计的优势仅集中在一个站点，按机构聚类后不具确证性；名义95%的插件经验贝叶斯后验预测计数区间实际覆盖率仅87.0%，在最难机构更低。

Conclusion: 参考一致性必须按站点和具体接口重新评估，不能跨机构转移。该研究涉及的是与机构标签的一致性，而非临床正确性，因此结果不能直接解读为临床效能。

Abstract: Vision-language models return structured chest-radiograph findings through interfaces exposing no confidence score, so a receiving institution cannot read off how far to trust an individual judgment. Whether agreement with an institution's reference standard transfers across sites, findings, prediction directions and question formats is largely unmeasured. We evaluated three generative vision-language models on three institutional chest-radiograph corpora and six findings under two elicitation protocols, comprising more than 345,000 finding-level predictions, and estimated finding-by-direction reference agreement at a receiving institution from a small budget of local labels. Estimation strategies were then stress-tested under repeated strict institution-held-out evaluation. Under evaluation excluding the receiving institution from development entirely, adaptive selection among the seven estimators that design admits did not improve on simple fixed alternatives: it achieved a mean Brier score of 0.1083, against 0.0853 for always using a Beta-Binomial empirical-Bayes estimator and 0.0855 for a target-only logistic model. Those two differ by 0.0003, less than this family's own sensitivity to a change of solver version, and each leads in about half the settings, so no default can be recommended. Their advantage over estimators pooling across institutions was concentrated at one site and not confirmatory once clustered by institution, and a plug-in empirical-Bayes posterior-predictive count interval at a nominal 95% level covered 87.0%, less at the hardest institution. Reference agreement therefore has to be re-evaluated per site and per interface; these results concern agreement with institutional labels, not clinical correctness.

</details>


### [7] [Impact of Dataset Composition on Embedded Real-Time UAV Wildfire Detection Using Compact YOLO Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07554)
*Eduardo de los Santos,Andre S. Kelbouscas,Ricardo B. Grando,Bruna V. Guterres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据集组成对嵌入式实时UAV野火检测系统的影响，使用紧凑型YOLO模型作为受控验证系列。通过对比四种训练配置（真实未增强、真实增强、混合未增强、混合增强），发现真实未增强数据集取得了最佳的整体操作点，合成数据混合和图像增强均未能改进最终部署性能，表明数据集真实性和域对齐比通过合成扩展增加训练集规模更有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的无人机野火检测系统发展受到可用的多样化真实世界训练图像有限的制约。本文旨在探究在资源受限的嵌入式部署条件下，合成数据混合和图像增强能否提升实际的检测性能，从而为数据集构建策略提供指导。

Method: 以紧凑型YOLO模型作为受控验证模型系列，设计了四种训练配置：真实未增强、真实增强、混合未增强（真实图像+AI生成图像）和混合增强。通过对比这些配置在嵌入式实时UAV野火检测任务中的性能，评估合成数据混合和数据增强的实际效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，真实未增强数据集取得了最佳的整体操作点，在召回率与平均精度均值（mAP）之间实现了最强平衡。合成数据混合和增强均未产生更优的最终部署选择。

Conclusion: 对于嵌入式UAV野火检测，数据集真实性和域对齐比通过合成扩展来增加训练集规模更具价值。建议在实际部署中优先保证数据的真实性和域适配性，而非盲目追求训练数据的数量。

Abstract: The development of vision-based wildfire detection systems for unmanned aerial vehicles is constrained by the limited availability of diverse real-world training images. This paper investigates the impact of dataset composition on embedded real-time UAV wildfire detection using compact YOLO models as a controlled validation family. Four training configurations were evaluated: real non-augmented, real augmented, hybrid non-augmented, and hybrid augmented, where the hybrid sets combine real wildfire images with AI-generated samples. The objective is to determine whether synthetic data mixing and image augmentation improve practical detection performance under resource-constrained deployment conditions. Experimental results show that the best overall operating point was obtained with the real non-augmented dataset, which achieved the strongest balance between recall and mean average precision for UAV-based wildfire detection. The results also show that neither hybridization with synthetic data nor augmentation produced a better final deployment choice. These findings suggest that, for embedded UAV wildfire detection, dataset realism and domain alignment are more valuable than increasing training set size through synthetic expansion.

</details>


### [8] [MVMD: A Multi-View Approach for Enhanced Mirror Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07559)
*Yidan Shen,Yu Wen,Chen Zhang,Xin Fu,Renjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVMD，一种用于多视图场景下的镜子检测方法，并构建了首个多视图镜子检测数据库。MVMD利用交叉注意力与自注意力机制，通过Inter-Views Block、Intra-View Block和Refinement Block三个关键模块，分别追踪镜中物体的视角偏移、检测镜内物体反射以及锐化镜子边界。实验表明，相比单图像镜子检测方法，MVMD在准确率上最高提升2.6%，IoU最高提升11.1%，尤其适用于镜子密集环境下的3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D重建中，镜子会引入严重的挑战，产生扭曲和碎片化的空间，导致3D模型不准确且不可靠。由于3D重建通常依赖多视图图像捕捉场景的不同视角，因此在进行重建之前检测并标注多视图图像中的镜子可以有效解决这一问题。然而现有方法仅关注单图像检测，忽视了多视图设置提供的丰富信息。

Method: MVMD的设计基于从不同视角观察到的物体与镜子内外反射物体之间的内在关联，通过交叉注意力和自注意力机制学习这些关系。模型由三个关键模块构成：Inter-Views Block用于追踪镜子内物体随视角变化而产生的位移；Intra-View Block用于检测镜子内物体的反射；Refinement Block用于锐化镜子边界并增强检测细节。此外，作者构建了首个专门用于多视图场景镜子检测的数据库。

Result: 实验结果表明，与单图像镜子检测技术相比，MVMD在准确率上最高提升2.6%，IoU最高提升11.1%，在镜子密集环境下的3D重建等计算机视觉任务中表现出显著的有效性。

Conclusion: MVMD通过充分利用多视图信息进行镜子检测，显著优于单图像方法，有效提升了镜子密集环境中3D重建的准确性，为计算机视觉中涉及镜子的场景理解提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In 3D reconstruction, mirrors introduce significant challenges by creating distorted and fragmented spaces, resulting in inaccurate and unreliable 3D models. As 3D reconstruction typically relies on multi-view images to capture different perspectives of a scene, detecting and labeling mirrors in multi-view images before reconstruction can effectively address this issue. However, existing methods focus solely on single-image detection, overlooking the rich information provided by multi-view setups. To overcome this limitation, we propose MVMD, a novel Multi-View Mirror Detection method, along with the first database specifically designed for mirror detection in multi-view scenes.
  The design of MVMD is grounded in the inherent associations between objects seen from different views and those reflected inside and outside of mirrors. These relationships are learned through cross- and self-attention mechanisms. MVMD consists of three key blocks: the Inter-Views Block tracks the shifts of objects within mirrors caused by changes in viewpoint; the Intra-View Block detects object reflections inside mirrors; and the Refinement Block sharpens mirror boundaries and enhances detected details.
  Experimental results show that our method improves accuracy by up to 2.6% and IoU by up to 11.1%, compared to single-image mirror detection techniques. This substantial improvement makes MVMD particularly effective for computer vision tasks, especially in enhancing the accuracy of 3D reconstruction in mirror-dense environments.

</details>


### [9] [XEns-CKD: An Explainable Ensemble-Based Approach for Chronic Kidney Disease Stage Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07561)
*Rehan Ahmad,Gousia Habib,Muhammad Shaban,Ishfaq Ahmad Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XEns-CKD，一种基于集成视觉变换器（ViT）的慢性肾脏病（CKD）分期分类方案，利用超声图像将肾脏功能分类为正常或五个CKD阶段。集成模型达到86.36%的分类准确率，并利用可解释AI技术（LIME、LRP、Attention-Min、Attention-Max）提高模型透明度和临床可信度，相比现有方法准确率提升了4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKD是一种"沉默"的疾病，其进展可能不会显著影响患者日常活动，但早期检测CKD分期可帮助患者了解肾功能状态并遵循医嘱延缓疾病进展。因此需要一种自动、准确的CKD分期分类方法。

Method: 作者提出XEns-CKD集成方案，由三个采用不同训练参数的ViT模型组成，在私有超声图像数据集上训练。性能评估使用宏观灵敏度、宏观特异性、宏观精确率、宏观F1分数、宏观Youden指数、Matthews相关系数（MCC）和宏观平衡准确率。此外，利用可解释AI技术（LIME、LRP、Attention-Min、Attention-Max）识别和解释CKD进展中受影响的肾脏区域，并将Attention-Min与Attention-Max结果结合生成注意力图。

Result: 集成模型总体分类准确率达到86.36%。提出的注意力图有效识别和解释了CKD从一个阶段进展到另一个阶段时受影响的肾脏区域，并突出了这些区域的CKD进展效应。与现有方法相比，提出的方法在五个CKD阶段和正常肾脏状态分类上实现了4%的准确率提升。

Conclusion: XEns-CKD通过集成多个ViT模型并结合可解释AI技术，实现了对慢性肾脏病分期的高精度超声图像分类，同时增强了模型透明度与临床可信度，相比现有方法具有4%的准确率优势，为CKD早期检测和进展监测提供了有效的辅助工具。

Abstract: Chronic kidney disease (CKD) is a silent disease. Its progression may not significantly hamper a person's daily routine. Human kidney function can be classified as normal or as one of the five stages of CKD. Early detection of the CKD stage can help patients understand the functional status of their kidneys and follow medical advice to slow CKD progression. In this paper, we propose XEns-CKD, a novel ensemble vision transformer-based scheme for CKD stage classification using ultrasound images. Three ViTs were trained on a private ultrasound image dataset using different training parameters. The performance of each ViT was evaluated using macro sensitivity, macro specificity, macro precision, macro F1-score, macro Youden index, the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), and macro balanced accuracy. The ensemble model achieved an overall classification accuracy of 86.36%. This work also emphasizes identifying and interpreting kidney regions affected by CKD progression. Explainable artificial intelligence techniques, including LIME, LRP, Attention-Min, and Attention-Max, were used to improve model transparency and clinical trust. An attention map combining the Attention-Min and Attention-Max results effectively identified and interpreted kidney regions affected during CKD progression from one stage to another. The attention map also highlighted the effects of CKD progression in these regions. Compared with existing methods, the proposed method classified the five CKD stages and normal kidney status with a 4% improvement in accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [Mechanistic Interpretability-Guided Selective Fine-Tuning of Vision-Language Models for Centimeter-Level Flood Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07562)
*Nafis Fuad,Xiaodong Qian,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FloodLlama系列视觉语言模型，用于从街道图像进行连续洪水深度估计（厘米级精度）。包括全微调的FloodLlama-Dense及两个基于机制可解释性分析的稀疏变体FloodLlama-MI5/MI6，后者仅微调少数因果相关的交叉注意力层，将可训练参数减少86-88%，同时保持近似精度。在真实世界基准上显著优于现有STURM-FloodDepth基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市洪水对交通基础设施构成日益严重的威胁，但当前没有任何运行系统能够在街道层面以厘米级分辨率提供实时洪水深度估计，这一技术空白亟需填补。

Method: 提出三种基于视觉语言模型的洪水深度估计模型：(1) FloodLlama-Dense为全参数微调的QLoRA基线；(2) 通过机制可解释性分析（线性探测、logit lens、CKA、交叉注意力熵）识别出两阶段适应模式及因果相关的交叉注意力层；(3) FloodLlama-MI5/MI6仅微调前五或六个因果相关交叉注意力层，大幅减少可训练参数。训练数据来自Unreal Engine 5生成的约281万张合成图像子集（约61万张），涵盖7种车辆类型、4种天气条件及0-40cm洪水深度。

Result: FloodLlama-Dense在合成基准上MAE为0.40cm，RMSE为1.97cm，Acc@5cm达97.59%。FloodLlama-MI5/MI6仅用6.55-7.86百万可训练参数（相比54.4百万减少86-88%）即可达到近似精度。在真实世界基准上，FloodLlama-MI6达98.62%准确率，显著优于STURM-FloodDepth基线的86.61%。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性分析揭示的层级适应模式可有效指导参数高效的洪水深度估计模型设计，稀疏微调方案在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为部署于现实街道洪水监测系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Urban flooding poses an escalating threat to transportation infrastructure, yet no operational system provides real-time, street-level flood-depth estimates at centimeter resolution. This paper presents three vision-language models fine-tuned for continuous flood-depth estimation from street-level imagery: FloodLlama-Dense, a fully fine-tuned QLoRA baseline, and FloodLlama-MI5 and FloodLlama-MI6, interpretability-guided sparse variants that fine-tune only the top five and six causally relevant cross-attention layers identified through mechanistic interpretability analysis, respectively. Training uses an approximately 610,000-image subset of a 2.81-million-image synthetic corpus generated in Unreal Engine 5. The dataset combines single-vehicle subsets with 5 cm depth increments and mixed-vehicle subsets with 1 cm depth increments, spanning seven vehicle types, four weather conditions, and flood depths from 0 to 40 cm. FloodLlama-Dense achieves an MAE of 0.40 cm, an RMSE of 1.97 cm, and an Acc@5cm of 97.59%. Mechanistic interpretability analysis combining linear probing, logit lens, centered kernel alignment (CKA), and cross-attention entropy reveals a two-stage adaptation pattern: layers L13-L22 restructure visual representations, while depth first becomes linearly decodable at layer L23. FloodLlama-MI5 and FloodLlama-MI6 leverage this insight by fine-tuning only five or six of the eight cross-attention layers, achieving an 86-88% reduction in trainable parameters (6.55-7.86 million versus 54.4 million) with minimal accuracy loss. On a real-world benchmark, FloodLlama-MI6 achieves 98.62% accuracy, compared with 86.61% for the published STURM-FloodDepth baseline.

</details>


### [11] [What to Edit Next: Visually Aligned Image-Editing Follow-Up Suggestions in Conversational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07565)
*Zhijing Zhang,Jinpeng Yu,Xin Song,Bingnan Li,Chuyue Li,Changhui Du,Xiaolin Fang,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三阶段框架，用于在图像创建对话中推荐后续编辑建议。该框架利用真实在线数据构建意图表并微调多模态策略（阶段1），通过用户点击反馈进行多目标强化学习优化（阶段2），并引入视觉验证器减少建议与当前图像的视觉不一致（阶段3）。在线A/B测试显示，视觉不一致率从3.7%降至0.9%，推荐CTR提升32.70%，图像采用率提升16.32%，每用户平均对话轮次提升39.90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式助手主要聚焦文本交互，图像创建场景中的后续编辑推荐尚未被充分探索。然而在图像创建任务中，有用的编辑建议必须反映用户偏好、提供多样化方向且在当前图像上可执行。作者从Qwen App收集了10万个真实多轮图像创建对话样本，发现80.1%的样本依赖于图像内容，突显了多模态推荐的必要性。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：阶段1利用真实在线数据构建经过人工审核的后续编辑意图表，生成SFT目标并微调多模态策略；阶段2利用用户点击反馈，通过多目标强化学习将规则引导的SFT建议与实际用户选择对齐；阶段3引入视觉验证器作为额外训练监督，减少建议编辑与当前图像之间的视觉不一致。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架在自动评估和人工评估上均显著优于基线。在数百万用户的在线随机A/B测试中，视觉不一致率从3.7%降至0.9%；推荐CTR提升32.70%，图像采用率提升16.32%，每用户平均对话轮次提升39.90%（均p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，三阶段框架能够有效解决图像创建场景中的后续编辑推荐问题，通过融合真实数据驱动的意图建模、用户反馈对齐的强化学习以及视觉一致性验证，实现了显著的效果提升，验证了多模态推荐在图像创作对话中的价值和可行性。

Abstract: Conversational assistants increasingly recommend follow-up edits to help users continue a task. Existing systems primarily target text-only interactions, leaving image-creation conversations underexplored. In image-creation tasks, useful follow-up edit suggestions must reflect user preferences, offer diverse directions, and remain executable on the current image. We collected 100,000 real multi-turn image-creation conversation samples from Qwen App and found that 80.1% are image-dependent, underscoring the need for multimodal recommendation. We address this setting with a three-stage framework. In Stage 1, we use real online data to build a human-reviewed table of appropriate follow-up editing intents, then create SFT targets and fine-tune a multimodal policy. In Stage 2, to align rule-guided SFT suggestions with actual user choices, we use user click feedback to optimize the policy through multi-objective reinforcement learning. In Stage 3, to reduce visual inconsistencies between suggested edits and the current image, we introduce a visual verifier as additional training supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms baselines on both automatic and human evaluations. In a live user-randomized A/B test with millions of users, our final framework reduces visual inconsistency from 3.7% to 0.9%. Furthermore, it significantly improves recommendation CTR by 32.70%, image take-away rate by 16.32%, and average conversation turns per user by 39.90% (all p<0.05).

</details>


### [12] [Temporal Generalization in fNIRS-Based Autism Classification: A Cross-Time-Window Transfer Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07567)
*Marios Petrov,Sahana Vinayak,Targol Bakhtiarvand,Moses Smith Guddah,Adham Atyabi,Frederick Shic,Kevin A. Pelphrey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化了fNIRS用于ASD分类中的跨时间窗迁移问题，即最优观测窗口因个体血流动力学延迟和神经血管耦合差异而变化。通过基准测试发现，零样本跨窗口准确率接近随机水平（54-69%），约5%的受试者特定微调可恢复至90-96%，域对抗与自监督策略无需目标数据即可达到78-90%，且短至2.5秒的窗口仍包含判别信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fNIRS-ASD分类方法假设时间对齐评估，但实际中不同受试者的血流动力学延迟和神经血管耦合存在差异，导致最优观测窗口因人而异，形成时间分布偏移并降低分类性能。作者旨在形式化并解决这一此前被忽视的跨时间窗迁移问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为跨时间窗迁移问题，引入在生物运动试验中变化窗口长度（2.5-10秒）和偏移量的协议。将fNIRS记录转换为地形图表示，在留一受试者交叉验证（N=124）下，对三种视觉架构进行两种零样本基线和八种域适应策略的基准测试。

Result: (1) 零样本跨窗口准确率接近随机（54-69%）；(2) 约5%的受试者特定微调达到90-96%，而受试者特定上限为97-100%，表明受试者间变异性是主要障碍；(3) 域对抗和自监督策略在无需目标受试者数据下达到78-90%；(4) 短至2.5秒的窗口仍可恢复判别信息。

Conclusion: 这些发现为在实际时间变异性条件下部署基于fNIRS的ASD分类器提供了实用路线图，明确了受试者间变异性是核心瓶颈，并展示了域适应方法在不依赖目标数据情况下的可行性。

Abstract: Functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) is a promising modality for autism spectrum disorder (ASD) classification, yet existing approaches assume temporally aligned evaluation. In practice, the optimal observation window varies across subjects due to differences in hemodynamic delay and neurovascular coupling, creating a temporal distribution shift that degrades performance. We formalize this as a \textit{cross-time-window transfer problem}, introducing a protocol that varies window length (2.5--10\,s) and offset within biological motion trials. Using topographic map representations of fNIRS recordings, we benchmark three vision architectures under two zero-shot baselines and eight adaptation strategies under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation ($N{=}124$). Key findings: (1) zero-shot cross-window accuracy is near chance (54--69\%); (2) ${\approx}5\%$ subject-specific fine-tuning recovers 90--96\%, while a subject-specific upper bound reaches 97--100\%, identifying inter-subject variability as the dominant barrier; (3) domain-adversarial and self-supervised strategies achieve 78--90\% without target-subject data; and (4) discriminative information is recoverable from windows as short as 2.5\,s. These findings provide a practical roadmap for deploying fNIRS-based ASD classifiers under realistic temporal variability.

</details>


### [13] [Latent-Frequency Validity: Fast Spectral Editing with Screened Video-VAE Transfer Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07569)
*Bowen Xue,Jiafeng Xiong,Xin Quan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出潜伏频率有效性（LFV）学习方法，在video-VAE潜空间中直接进行频谱编辑，无需解码-滤波-重编码流程。LFV学习紧凑的VAE特定频谱响应，并通过验证选择的路径（从对角逐频率校准器C1到全通道混合CM）实现跨通道容量作为可控的逐编辑资源。实验覆盖544个VAE-编辑单元，423个廉价算子中277个由C1处理、146个需要通道混合，所有跨域测试均通过验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在video-VAE潜空间做频谱编辑可避免昂贵的解码-滤波-重编码流程，但存在两个关键挑战：video-VAE会跨通道重新分配像素空间的频带，导致潜空间编辑难以精确控制；同时潜空间编辑可能破坏VAE的往返（round-trip）动力学，导致解码质量下降。现有方法缺乏对这些问题系统性、可验证的解决方案。

Method: 提出潜伏频率有效性（LFV）框架：学习紧凑的VAE特定频谱响应，仅在能提升解码目标保真度且不加剧往返漂移时启用。LFV通过验证选择的路径从对角逐频率校准器（C1）逐步推进到全通道混合（CM），将跨通道容量视为可控资源，按编辑需求动态分配。

Result: 在544个VAE-编辑单元（六种频谱族）中，LFV发出423个廉价算子：277个由C1处理，146个（34.5%）需通道混合。径向扫描120单元中99/100通过的源视频分组留出评估；额外五种滤波族中全部323个算子通过留出评估。全冻结OpenVid拟合算子（含验证选择路径系数）在20个CogVideoX和HunyuanVideo生成域单元上全部通过。延迟匹配直接潜滤波，比像素滤波-重编码快约3倍。

Conclusion: LFV证明了在潜空间直接进行可控频谱编辑的可行性，其验证驱动的资源分配策略有效平衡了编辑精度与计算开销。频谱响应图揭示了不同VAE的独特行为模式，包括CogVideoX的强通道耦合响应和Open-Sora的高频稳定性边界，为后续video-VAE潜空间操作提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Direct spectral editing in video-VAE latents can control noise, flicker, smoothness, and frequency content without a decode--filter--reencode pass. However, video VAEs may redistribute pixel-space frequency bands across latent channels, and latent edits can disrupt VAE round-trip dynamics. We introduce \emph{latent-frequency validity} (LFV), which learns a compact VAE-specific spectral response and deploys it only when it improves decoded-target fidelity without worsening round-trip drift. LFV follows a validation-selected path from a diagonal per-frequency calibrator (C1) to full channel mixing (CM), making cross-channel capacity a controllable per-edit resource. Across 544 VAE--edit cells spanning six spectral families, LFV emits 423 cheap operators: 277 are handled by C1, while 146 (34.5\% of emitted operators) require channel mixing. On the primary 120-cell radial sweep, 99/100 emitted operators pass source-video-grouped held-out evaluation. Across five additional filter families, all 323 emitted operators pass held-out evaluation. Fully frozen OpenVid-fitted operators, including the validation-selected path coefficient, pass all 20 tested CogVideoX and HunyuanVideo generated-domain cells without adaptation. The selected response matches direct latent-filter latency and is about $3\times$ faster than pixel filter--reencode. The resulting maps reveal distinct VAE regimes, including strongly channel-coupled CogVideoX responses and a sharp Open-Sora high-band stability frontier.

</details>


### [14] [COMEX: A Composition-Grounded Benchmark and Learning Framework for Explainable Aesthetic Image Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07570)
*Rui Yang,Wei Zhou,Dingyong Gou,Xiaohui Cui,Cong Li,Yinyin Gong,Yipo Huang,Jiliang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将可解释的美学图像裁剪重构为"裁剪-构图-解释"三合一的结构化任务，并构建了包含33,161个四元组的COMEX基准数据集（包含扩展图像、裁剪框、构图类别和基于构图的解释），同时提出两阶段SFT+GRPO训练框架，在15个大型视觉语言模型上建立了全面的测试基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有crop-and-explain方法将解释视为事后文本生成，忽略了构图这一将裁剪决策与可理解推理联系起来的核心美学因素，缺乏结构化的、基于构图的可解释裁剪框架。

Method: 1) 将任务重新定义为结构化的crop-composition-explanation问题；2) 构建COMEX基准数据集，通过图像扩展和IO-reversal流水线生成33,161个四元组；3) 提出两阶段SFT+GRPO训练框架：监督微调阶段建立结构化输出协议和基础裁剪能力，GRPO阶段进一步优化裁剪质量、构图预测和解释忠实度。

Result: 在COMEX上对15个大型视觉语言模型和现有裁剪方法进行基准测试，实验表明所提出的框架在COMEX及先前基准上均表现出优异的性能和良好的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于构图的可解释美学裁剪框架和COMEX基准为联合学习裁剪定位、构图理解和解释生成提供了有效的解决方案，展示了强大的跨基准泛化能力。

Abstract: Explainable aesthetic image cropping requires not only localizing a visually pleasing crop but also explaining why it is preferred. Existing crop-and-explain methods largely treat explanation as post-hoc text generation and overlook composition, a key aesthetic factor that links crop decisions with interpretable reasoning. In this paper, we reformulate explainable aesthetic image cropping as a structured crop-composition-explanation problem. To support this setting, we introduce COMEX, a new benchmark built through image expansion and an IO-reversal pipeline. COMEX contains 33,161 quadruples, each consisting of an expanded image, a crop box, a composition category, and a composition-grounded explanation, enabling joint learning of crop localization, composition understanding, and explanation generation. We further propose a two-stage SFT+GRPO framework, where supervised fine-tuning establishes the structured output protocol and basic cropping ability, and GRPO further improves crop quality, composition prediction, and explanation faithfulness. We benchmark 15 large vision-language models and existing cropping methods on COMEX, establishing a comprehensive testbed for composition-grounded explainable aesthetic cropping. Experiments on both COMEX and prior benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and transferability of our framework, with strong performance across evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [15] [A Review of Vision-Based Vehicle Detection for UAV-Based Traffic Monitoring: Experimental Insights and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07571)
*Jianlin Ye,Christos Kyrkou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于无人机（UAV）的交通监控系统在智能交通系统（ITS）中的应用进展，重点聚焦于用于交通分析的深度神经网络模型。论文识别了三大核心挑战：与交通控制系统的兼容性、实时处理能力以及不同环境条件下的鲁棒检测，并指出现有解决方案缺乏利用无人机数据响应事故和管理交通的综合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习显著提升了无人机交通监控的检测精度，但在实际部署中仍需对准确性、延迟性以及与现有交通系统的协同性进行严格评估。现有研究往往缺乏综合框架，无法有效利用无人机采集的数据来响应交通事故和管理交通流，因此需要系统性综述来指导未来研究。

Method: 本文采用系统性文献综述的方法，回顾了近期在基于无人机交通监控领域的研究进展，重点分析深度神经网络模型在不同城市环境中的交通分析应用，并归纳总结文献中反复出现的三大核心挑战。

Result: 识别出三个主要挑战：与交通控制系统的兼容性、实现实时处理以优化交通流、以及在不同环境条件下保持鲁棒检测。同时指出现有解决方案缺乏综合利用无人机数据进行事故响应和交通管理的完整框架。

Conclusion: 未来研究应聚焦于优化检测模型、边缘计算处理以及自适应控制集成，以提升城市交通管理的响应能力。无人机监控虽然有广阔前景，但需要更全面的架构设计来弥合感知与决策之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: In Intelligent Transportation System (ITS), unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based surveillance offers an innovative solution to traffic surveillance with wide coverage and real-time data collection capabilities. In comparison to fixed ground-based infrastructure, UAVs are able to respond to dynamic traffic but present challenges such as vehicle detection at varying altitudes, compensation for motion-induced image variations and efficient processing of high-resolution images. Deep learning has been largely beneficial on improving the detection accuracy; however, for practical deployment, a critical assessment of the accuracy, latency, and harmonization with current transportation systems needs to be carefully considered. This survey reviews recent advancements in the UAV-based traffic monitoring, with a primary focus being deep neural network models for traffic analytics in various urban settings. Three main challenges identified in the literature are ensuring compatibility with traffic control systems, achieving real-time processing to optimize traffic flow, and maintaining robust detection in different environmental conditions. Existing solutions often lack comprehensive frameworks for utilizing UAV captured data to respond to incidents and manage traffic effectively. Future research should focus on optimal detection models, edge processing, and adaptive control integration to improve the responsiveness of urban traffic management.

</details>


### [16] [BRACE: Taming Sharp Irregularities via Barycentric Rational Forecasting for Fast Diffusion Transformers Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07572)
*Jinlong Yang,Jinke Wu,Lizilin,Yao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BRACE提出了一种基于重心有理函数(结合Chebyshev权重)的时序特征缓存预测方法,用于替代现有基于导数多项式外推的缓存预测方案,在高效视频/图像生成中实现更稳定、更高质量的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)的时序特征缓存方法(cache-then-forecast)依赖基于导数的多项式外推,在高加速比下因长期预测不稳定而导致严重的生成质量下降。作者观察到DiT特征轨迹虽然全局平滑,但频繁出现尖锐不规则和局部非平滑现象,这催生了从导数驱动外推转向特征驱动有理预测的新思路。

Method: BRACE维护一个局部滑动窗口来缓存稀疏的历史特征,并利用自适应的Chebyshev权重构建重心有理(barycentric rational)函数,直接聚合原始特征以保障数值稳定性,从而完成对冗余计算的跳过与时序预测。

Result: 大量实验表明,BRACE在多种DiT架构上取得了最优的质量-效率权衡,且仅引入可忽略的计算开销。

Conclusion: BRACE通过将预测范式从导数驱动的多项式外推转变为特征驱动的重心有理预测,有效解决了高加速场景下DiT特征缓存预测的稳定性瓶颈,实现了先进的生成质量与计算效率平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have demonstrated exceptional performance in high-fidelity image and video generation. To alleviate their massive computational overhead, temporal feature caching has been proposed to bypass redundant computations. However, existing cache-then-forecast methods driven by derivative-based polynomials often cause severe quality degradation under high acceleration due to unstable long-step predictions. To address this bottleneck, we propose Barycentric Rational Forecasting with Chebyshev Enhancement (BRACE). Motivated by the observation that DiT feature trajectories are globally smooth yet frequently exhibit sharp irregularities and local non-smoothness, BRACE shifts the paradigm from derivative-driven polynomial extrapolation to feature-driven rational forecasting. Specifically, it maintains a local sliding window to cache sparse historical features and leverages adapted Chebyshev weights to formulate a barycentric rational function, directly aggregating these raw features to ensure numerical stability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BRACE achieves state-of-the-art quality-efficiency trade-offs across various DiT architectures with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [17] [Multimodal Skin Lesion Classification with Swin Transformer and Clinical Metadata Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07574)
*Nethmi Pathirana,Isuru Munasinghe,Dileeka Alwis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合Swin Transformer图像特征与结构化临床元数据的多模态分类框架，用于皮肤病变分类。在公开数据集上达到92.55%的测试准确率和91.33%的宏F1分数，并通过温度缩放校准降低了期望校准误差，同时引入不确定性估计和可解释性分析，实现了可靠且可信任的自动化皮肤病变诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病变分类对皮肤癌早期诊断至关重要，但皮镜图像中的类别不平衡、类间相似性和类内变异使得自动化分析仍具挑战性，需要更鲁棒的分类框架来提升诊断性能。

Method: 提出多模态融合框架：1) 使用Swin Transformer提取皮肤镜图像特征；2) 将图像特征与结构化临床元数据结合，实现视觉-上下文联合学习；3) 采用温度缩放作为后处理校准方法降低期望校准误差；4) 引入不确定性估计评估预测置信度；5) 进行定性可解释性分析。

Result: 公开数据集上测试准确率达92.55%，宏F1分数为91.33%，在少数类上表现优异。温度缩放有效降低了期望校准误差，提升了预测可靠性，可解释性分析显示模型聚焦于病变区域进行推理。

Conclusion: 多模态融合结合校准与可解释性分析，为自动化皮肤病变分类提供了一种有效且可信任的方法，在临床辅助诊断中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Skin lesion classification plays an important role in supporting the early diagnosis of skin cancer. However, automated analysis remains challenging due to class imbalance, inter-class similarity, and intra-class variability in dermoscopic images. This paper proposes a multimodal classification framework that combines Swin Transformer-based image features with structured clinical metadata to improve diagnostic performance through integrated visual-context learning. Experiments on a publicly available dataset show that the proposed model achieves a test accuracy of 92.55% and a macro F1-score of 91.33%, with strong performance across minority classes. Temperature scaling is applied as a post-hoc calibration method, resulting in a reduction in expected calibration error and improving prediction reliability, while uncertainty estimation is incorporated to further assess the confidence of model predictions. Qualitative explainability analysis further shows that the model focuses on lesion regions during inference. Therefore, the results demonstrate that multimodal fusion, combined with calibration and interpretability analysis, provides an effective and trustworthy approach for automated skin lesion classification.

</details>


### [18] [Beyond Isotropic Assumptions: Continuity-Constrained Segmentation and GPU Morphometry for Nanoscale GBM Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07575)
*Arash Fatehi,Robin Ebbestad,Linus Butt,Hans Blom,Sigrid Lundberg,Hannes Olauson,Hjalmar Brismar,David Unnersjö-Jess,Thomas Benzing,Katarzyna Bozek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端、GPU加速的框架，无需额外标注即可在原始各向异性采集体积上直接训练分割模型，通过随机旋转训练补丁（利用横向平面提供轴向信息）和z轴连续性损失来克服轴向欠采样问题，并应用于肾小球基底膜（GBM）的三维形态学量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共聚焦显微镜采集的光学透明化、膨胀组织图像在轴向方向欠采样，导致三维结构在轴向出现不连续，阻碍重建和自动定量分析。传统解决方法是将轴向维度上采样为各向同性体积后再训练分割模型，但需要在上采样空间中做密集标注，标注负担过重。

Method: 1) 直接在原始采集体积上训练模型，避免额外的各向同性标注；2) 随机旋转训练补丁，利用分辨率良好的横向平面来补充缺失的轴向信息；3) 引入z轴连续性损失，保持相邻切片的一致性；4) 适配卷积（3D U-Net）和Transformer（SwinUNETR）两种骨干网络；5) 通过Gaussian共识聚合重叠补丁；6) 在GPU上通过射线-表面相交计算经点扩散函数校正的膜厚度。

Result: 分割精度达到专家间一致性水平；连续性感知训练改善了重建平滑度，抑制了周期性台阶状伪影，仅以很小的精度代价换取。该方法成功量化了GBM厚度在三维重建表面的变化，并捕捉到了疾病相关的增厚。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无需密集体素标注或图像恢复的全自动各向异性三维形态计量，能够对生物结构进行三维定量分析，适用于疾病相关的形态学变化检测。

Abstract: Confocal microscopy of optically cleared and swelled tissue resolves complex biological structures in 3D, but such acquisitions are highly anisotropic: along the under-sampled axial direction the structure can appear discontinuous, hampering reconstruction and automated quantitative analysis. The usual remedy upsamples the axial dimension to an isotropic volume before training a segmentation model, which requires dense annotations in the upsampled space, a prohibitive labeling burden.
  We present an end-to-end, GPU-accelerated framework that overcomes this without additional annotations. The model is trained on the native acquisition volume; random rotation of training patches leverages the well-resolved lateral plane to supply the missing axial information, and a z-axis continuity loss keeps neighboring slices consistent. We adapt both a convolutional (3D U-Net) and a transformer (SwinUNETR) backbone, aggregate overlapping patches by Gaussian consensus, and compute point-spread-function-corrected membrane thickness by ray-surface intersection on the GPU.
  We apply the method to the glomerular basement membrane (GBM), a thin, highly convoluted part of the kidney's filtration barrier that grows more irregular in disease. Segmentation accuracy matches inter-expert agreement. Continuity-aware training improves reconstruction smoothness and suppresses a periodic terracing artifact at minimal accuracy cost. We quantify GBM thickness across the reconstructed 3D surface and capture disease-related thickening, enabling fully automated anisotropic 3D morphometry of biological structures without dense volumetric labels or image restoration.

</details>


### [19] [Open-World Hierarchical Perception: Taxonomic Abstraction over Class-Agnostic Proposals for the Safe Handling of Out-of-Vocabulary Road Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07577)
*Felix Schaller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种开放世界感知层：将层次化分类学抽象叠加在类无关区域提议之上，使自动驾驶闭集检测器无法框出的物体也能被分类或标记。在COCO留七类基准上，扁平闭集检测器对235个真实标注目标100%给出错误的具体标签（37%超类错误），而层次化层做到零置信的错误具体标签，安全处理94%的物体，但正确超类恢复率仅26%，其余69%被保守标记为未知。作者明确这是安全性结果而非特异性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶闭集检测器对类别集合外的物体（如马车、道路杂物、牲畜）只能给出错误的具体标签或丢弃该物体。前人工作将平坦标签集替换为层次化分类学并加运行时抽象规则，但仅在闭集检测器已产生的边界框上评估。本文的动机是将层次化抽象置于类无关区域提议之上，使检测器从未框出的物体也能被分类或标记，并弥补前人无法做到的真实标注留类评估。

Method: 本文将分类学抽象层放置于类无关区域提议之上，实现开放世界感知。研究三种开放世界信号：类无关分割、基于外观的分布外评分、单目深度，论证单一2D线索不足及组合方式。评估方法为ground-truth留类基准：从COCO中排除七个类，对235个真实标注裁剪进行分类测试。

Result: 在留出七类COCO的基准上：扁平闭集头对235个目标100%给出错误的具体标签，其中37%错误超类（如动物被命名为车辆）；层次化层产生零置信错误具体标签，安全处理94%物体（正确超类或明确的UNKNOWN OBSTACLE）。但正确超类仅恢复26%，剩余69%被保守标记为未知。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是一个开放世界感知层，对词表外物体永不做出置信的类别错误判断，同时诚实报告其代价——这是安全性结果而非特异性结果，正确超类恢复率较低但避免了灾难性的错误分类。

Abstract: A closed-set detector for autonomous driving must assign every object one of a fixed set of labels. On an object outside that set (a horse-drawn carriage, road debris, livestock on a rural road) it can only force a confident but wrong specific label or drop the object. Prior work in this series replaced the flat label set with a hierarchical taxonomy and a runtime abstraction rule, but evaluated it only on the boxes a closed detector already produces. This paper takes the layer open-world: we place taxonomic abstraction on top of class-agnostic region proposals so objects the closed detector never boxes can still be classified or flagged; we report a feasibility study of three open-world signals (class-agnostic segmentation, appearance-based out-of-distribution scoring, monocular depth) that shows why no single 2D cue suffices and how they compose; and we run the evaluation the earlier papers could not, a ground-truth leave-classes-out benchmark on real annotated objects. Holding out seven COCO classes and classifying their 235 ground-truth crops, a flat closed head emits a confident wrong specific label 100% of the time (37% of them in the wrong super-category, e.g. an animal named as a vehicle), whereas the hierarchical layer emits zero confident wrong specific labels and safely handles 94% of the objects (a correct super-category, or an explicit UNKNOWN OBSTACLE). We are explicit that this is a safety result, not a specificity one: the correct super-category is recovered only 26% of the time and the remaining 69% are conservatively flagged unknown. The contribution is an open-world perception layer that never makes a confident categorical mistake on an out-of-vocabulary object, together with an honest account of its cost.

</details>


### [20] [Geometry Beats Estimated Depth: RGB-Only Multi-Camera 3D Tracking under Sim2Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07579)
*Abdullah Naeem,Anav Katwal,Ayon Dey,Noman Khan,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1中多摄像头室内3D感知的Sim2Real设置，对比了几何优先管线与伪LiDAR（单目深度）管线，发现几何优先管线以13.0 3D HOTA显著优于伪LiDAR的0.12，证明跨视角几何一致性而非单目深度精度是主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证一个核心假设：在Sim2Real设置下，跨视角几何一致性比单目深度精度更能决定多摄像头3D感知性能。通过受控对比实验检验该假设，并为RGB-only推断提供可行基线。

Method: 构建两条RGB-only管线：(1)几何优先管线——YOLO11x检测、单应性升维到世界坐标系、类别级3D尺寸先验、多摄像头融合、世界坐标跟踪及离线轨迹拼接；(2)伪LiDAR管线——D4RT和Metric3D v2单目深度反投影为融合点云后送入V-DETR 3D检测器。并进行全面消融实验验证各组件贡献。

Result: 几何优先管线达到13.0 3D HOTA（51.6 LocA），伪LiDAR仅0.12（9.2 LocA）。跨视图单目深度不一致性导致伪LiDAR崩溃，尺度校正必要但不充分，领域自适应微调在预算内无法修复。消融显示仅离线拼接有效，SAHI检测、外观Re-ID、学习升维、RT-DETR集成、TTA和域随机化均无效。瓶颈互补：检测质量限制几何路线（DetA），定位一致性限制伪LiDAR（LocA）。

Conclusion: 跨视图几何一致性是Sim2Real多摄像头3D感知的关键约束，单目深度在此设置下不可靠。几何优先管线作为可复现的RGB-only解决方案有效，且检测质量与定位一致性是两条路线的互补瓶颈。

Abstract: The AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1 evaluates multi-camera 3D perception in large indoor warehouses under a synthetic-to-real (Sim2Real) setting; depth is available only for training and validation, so inference is RGB-only. We use two RGB-only routes as a controlled test of one hypothesis: that cross-view geometric consistency, not monocular depth accuracy, governs performance under Sim2Real. The first is a geometry-first pipeline: YOLO11x detection, homography lifting to the world frame, class-level 3D size priors, multi-camera fusion, world-coordinate tracking, and offline tracklet stitching. The second is estimated-depth pseudo-LiDAR: monocular depth (D4RT, Metric3D~v2) back-projected into a fused point cloud and passed to a 3D detector (V-DETR), mirroring prior point-cloud winners that used depth. The gap is decisive: geometry-first reaches 13.0 3D HOTA (51.6 LocA), whereas pseudo-LiDAR collapses to 0.12 (9.2 LocA). We trace the collapse to cross-view inconsistency of monocular depth---scale correction is necessary but not sufficient---which domain-adaptation fine-tuning does not repair within budget. Within the geometry pipeline, offline stitching is the only intervention that helps; SAHI detection, appearance Re-ID, learned lifting, RT-DETR ensembling, test-time augmentation, and domain randomization all fail to beat the baseline detector. The bottlenecks are complementary: detection quality bounds the geometry route (DetA), localization consistency bounds pseudo-LiDAR (LocA). We release a complete, reproducible RGB-only pipeline and ablation.

</details>

*William R. Keely,Philip G. Brodrick,Katherine Mistick,Adam Chlus,Robert O. Green,Philip E. Dennison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全物理正向模型的野火温度反演框架，应用于NASA AVIRIS-3 VSWIR成像光谱数据，利用GPU加速的非线性最小二乘方法实现飞行周期内的快速收敛，在2025年FireSense AVIRIS-3任务中验证了其有效性和可推广性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火温度是理解燃烧动态和火灾行为的核心参数，但现有遥感方法在温度反演精度和实时性方面存在不足。需要一种能够同时解算太阳辐射和火源发射辐射、并能在飞行时间尺度内完成反演的框架，以支持高效的野火监测。

Method: 采用全物理正向模型方法，在残差拟合中使用完整光谱范围，同时解算来自温度分布的太阳辐射和发射辐射；利用基于GPU的先进非线性最小二乘算法加速前向模型反演收敛，实现飞行节奏内的有效火温估计；并在粗化空间分辨率下验证后验参数可推广到EMIT等星载成像光谱仪。

Result: 在注入热信号的模拟光谱上验证得到RMSE为41.8 K；对2025年FireSense AVIRIS-3任务的168次含活跃野火光谱的过境数据进行反演，短波红外（SWIR）波段残差辐射拟合≤10%；在粗化空间分辨率下，后验分布对亚像元温度范围覆盖良好，各分位数绝对误差为30 K，空间分辨率之间的平均绝对误差为27.16 K。

Conclusion: 该反演框架在AVIRIS-3数据上实现了高精度的野火温度反演，并具备实时处理能力，且反演的后验参数能够很好地推广到EMIT等星载成像光谱仪，验证了该方法的通用性和星载应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we present a wildfire temperature retrieval framework for VSWIR imaging spectroscopy data, employed on data from NASA's Airborne Visible Infrared Imaging Spectrometer (AVIRIS-3). The retrieval framework utilizes a full-physics approach in which a forward model is employed to resolve both solar and emitted radiance derived from a temperature distribution and utilizes the full spectral range in the residual fit. To optimize the forward model retrieval, we use state-of-the-art nonlinear least squares methods implemented for fast convergence on the on-board GPU, allowing for estimation of effective fire temperature within flight cadence. We verify the forward model assumptions on simulated spectra with an injected thermal signature and find good agreement with an RMSE of $41.8$ Kelvin (K). We apply the retrieval over the full 2025 FireSense AVIRIS-3 campaign, totaling 168 overflights with probable active fire spectra, and demonstrate a residual radiance fit of $\leq 10\%$ across bands in the short-wave infrared (SWIR). Lastly, we verify the applicability of the retrieved posterior fire temperature parameters to generalize to space-borne imaging spectrometers such as EMIT, by retrieving at coarsened spatial resolution. We find that the posterior distribution exhibits good coverage of the underlying sub-pixel temperature range with an absolute error of $30$ K across quantiles and a mean absolute error of $27.16$ K between spatial resolutions.

</details>


### [22] [Multi-Branch Policy Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07581)
*Shuai Lyu,Yuning Gong,Ruiling Gao,Xiaoran Shang,Zhonghong Ou,Ping Zong,Yifan Zhu,Yuan Sun,Yang Qin,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多分支策略优化（MBPO），一种基于树结构的强化学习方法，用于多模态大语言模型的推理优化。该方法在视觉-语言决策边界构建推理树，通过分支间相对优势进行段级信用分配，并引入时间回放缓冲区提升样本复用效率。实验表明MBPO在多个多模态推理基准上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于组的强化学习方法对多模态大语言模型采用轨迹级的统一信用分配，即对响应中的所有token赋予同一优势值。然而多模态推理存在远高于纯文本场景的感知不确定性，模型需反复检查视觉信息以验证中间解释，不同的视觉基础会导致分歧的推理路径，使得统一信用分配不合适，并导致相对优势逐渐趋近于零。

Method: MBPO是一个基于树结构的框架，在视觉-语言决策边界构建推理树，使兄弟分支能够探索多样化的视觉假设；通过分支相对优势进行段级信用分配而非token级统一赋值；引入时间回放缓冲区以复用有信息量的片段，同时控制策略陈旧性。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上的实验表明，MBPO优于代表性的基线方法，同时提升了学习信号质量和优化效率。

Conclusion: MBPO通过段级信用分配和树结构探索有效解决了多模态推理中统一优势赋值退化的问题，为多模态大语言模型的强化学习优化提供了更高效的方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning methods for multimodal large language models typically rely on trajectory-level credit assignment that applies a single advantage to all tokens in a response. However, multimodal reasoning involves substantially higher perceptual uncertainty than text-only settings, where the model must repeatedly re-examine visual information to verify intermediate interpretations, and different visual groundings can lead to divergent reasoning paths, making such uniform credit assignment particularly inadequate and causing relative advantages to progressively degenerate toward zero. To address these challenges, we propose Multi-Branch Policy Optimization (MBPO), a tree-based framework that constructs reasoning trees at vision-language decision boundaries, enabling sibling branches to explore diverse visual hypotheses and assigning segment-level credit through branch-relative advantages. We further introduce a temporal replay buffer to reuse informative segments while controlling policy staleness. Experiments on several multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that MBPO outperforms representative baselines, improving both learning signal quality and optimization efficiency. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/ShuaiLyu0110/MBPO.

</details>

*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的预测性维护策略，利用热成像和电力传感器数据检测数据中心网络硬件（路由器、交换机、服务器）的早期故障迹象。通过生成模拟数据集（Normal/Warning/Critical三种状态），对比三种ImageNet预训练CNN模型（ResNet-50、InceptionV3、VGG16）与多模态CNN-LSTM融合模型，实验表明ROI预处理显著提升CNN性能（ResNet-50从52%提升至91%），而CNN-LSTM多模态融合模型达到94%的最高准确率，验证了领域特定预处理与传感器融合对早期故障预测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心网络硬件意外故障会导致服务中断和昂贵停机，需要能够提前检测设备故障迹象的预测性维护方案。现有方法难以利用多模态数据实现早期预警，因此需要探索结合热成像视觉信息与电力传感时间序列的深度学习解决方案。

Method: 生成模拟标注数据集（含热成像图像与功率读数，标记为Normal、Warning、Critical三种状态）；评估三种ImageNet预训练CNN模型（ResNet-50、InceptionV3、VGG16）；提出融合视觉信息与传感器时间序列的多模态CNN-LSTM模型；在有无预处理（ROI提取与归一化）条件下进行实验对比。

Result: 无预处理时CNN准确率中等（ResNet-50为52%）；ROI预处理显著提升性能（ResNet-50达91%）；CNN-LSTM多模态融合模型达到最高准确率94%，精确率和召回率接近95%，优于所有单一模态CNN模型。

Conclusion: 领域特定预处理（ROI提取）和多模态传感器融合能显著提升早期故障预测能力，为数据中心网络硬件提供基于非侵入式监控的主动预测性维护基础。

Abstract: Unplanned network hardware malfunctions can interrupt services and result in expensive downtime in data centers. A deep learning-based predictive maintenance strategy is presented that utilizes thermal imaging and power sensor data to detect early indicators of equipment breakdown in routers, switches, and servers. A simulated dataset was generated comprising annotated thermal pictures and power readings indicative of three operating states: Normal, Warning, and Critical. Three ImageNet-pretrained convolutional neural network (CNN) models ResNet-50, InceptionV3, and VGG16 were assessed together with a multi-modal CNN-LSTM fusion model that integrates visual and sensor time-series information. Experiments were performed with and without pre-processing procedures, including region-of-interest (ROI) extraction and normalization. In the absence of pre-processing, CNNs attained moderate accuracy (e.g., ResNet-50 at 52%), but ROI-based pre-processing significantly enhanced performance (ResNet-50 accuracy reaching 91%). The CNN-LSTM model attained the greatest accuracy of 94%, with precision and recall approaching 95%, illustrating the effectiveness of multi-modal fusion. The results validate that domain-specific pre-processing and sensor fusion substantially improve early failure prediction, providing a potential foundation for proactive maintenance of network hardware through non-intrusive monitoring.

</details>


### [24] [ComplexityWorld: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Verifiable Visual Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07584)
*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了COMPLEXITYWORLD基准，包含390个任务、39个领域视觉世界和29个决策类别，用于评估视觉语言模型在需要满足全局约束的完整决策任务上的能力。实验发现所有模型（除GPT-5.6-Sol外）在直接推理下的可验证接受率低于40%，揭示了视觉到决策的持续瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在视觉感知方面进展迅速，但许多真实世界任务不仅需要识别图像内容，还需要利用视觉证据做出满足全局约束的完整决策。现有基准缺乏对这种视觉-决策复合能力的系统评估，研究者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 构建COMPLEXITYWORLD基准：每个任务由隐藏的结构化规范生成，渲染为视觉场景，并由可执行的验证器对任意可行解进行评分。该基准覆盖39个领域启发的视觉世界和29个决策类别，共390个任务。

Result: 直接推理下，除GPT-5.6-Sol达到75.6%外，所有被评估模型的验证器接受率均低于40%。当相同的决策信息以结构化形式显式给出时，性能显著提升，但在等价视觉呈现下性能波动剧烈。智能体脚手架只带来较小且依赖模型的收益。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了持续的视觉到决策瓶颈，仅靠增加推理计算量无法消除该瓶颈。模型在视觉信息到全局约束决策之间的转换能力存在系统性不足。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made rapid progress in visual perception and increasingly support real-world tasks that depend on images. Many such tasks, however, require more than rec- ognizing what an image contains: a model must use visual evidence to make a complete decision whose parts jointly satisfy global constraints. We introduce COMPLEXITYWORLD, a benchmark of 390 tasks across 39 domain-inspired visual worlds and 29 decision categories. Each task is generated from a hidden structured specification, rendered as a visual scene, and scored by an exe- cutable verifier that accepts any feasible solution. Under direct inference, all evaluated models ex- cept GPT-5.6-Sol remain below 40% verifier ac- ceptance rate (VAR), while GPT-5.6-Sol reaches 75.6%. Performance improves substantially when the same decision information is made explicit in structured form, yet varies sharply across equiva- lent visual presentations. Agent scaffolds provide smaller, model-dependent gains. Together, these results reveal a persistent visual-to-decision bot- tleneck that additional inference alone does not remove.

</details>


### [25] [LAVE: Latent Visual Evidence-Enhanced Planning for Video Tool-use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07585)
*Zijian Wang,Junnan Zhu,Rongzhen Li,Xiao Liu,Guohui Xiang,Quan Lu,Lijia Liu,Yining Wang,Jiang Zhong,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAVE是一个无需训练的框架，通过双通道观察接口（可见通道保留文本轨迹，潜在通道存储工具调用的视觉更新）来解决视频工具智能体中的"工具观察瓶颈"问题，使智能体能够重用已完成工具调用的潜在视觉证据，在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和CG-Bench上持续提升视频工具使用智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解要求模型高效获取和重用稀疏视觉证据。现有视频工具使用智能体通过迭代调用多尺度视觉工具来解决此问题，但Tool-Planner通信仅依赖文本观察，这种纯文本接口对工具计算结果提供有损摘要，导致未被语言化的视觉证据被丢弃，无法用于后续规划。作者将此限制定义为"工具观察瓶颈"。

Method: 提出LAVE框架，采用双通道观察接口：可见通道保留原始文本轨迹，潜在通道存储预语言化的视觉更新及其工具角色、源帧时间戳和视觉位置。规划期间，LAVE检索与当前Planner状态相关但未被文本观察覆盖的证据，通过有界时间戳对齐的潜在更新和熵约束帧时间路由进行整合。该框架无需额外训练、帧重放或修改原始编排。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和CG-Bench上，LAVE在多个骨干网络上持续提升视频工具使用智能体的性能。在可比的帧预算下，LAVE在Video-MME整体得分上比最强基线提升3.76分。

Conclusion: LAVE证明了潜在视觉证据重用对于多步视频智能体规划的有效性，通过利用已有工具调用的潜在视觉信息，在无需训练的情况下显著提升长视频理解性能。

Abstract: Long-video understanding requires models to efficiently acquire and reuse sparse visual evidence from long and redundant video streams. Recent video tool-use agents address this challenge by iteratively invoking visual Tools at different temporal scales, but their Tool-Planner communication typically relies on textual observations. Such text-only interfaces provide lossy summaries of Tool computations, causing previously computed visual evidence not verbalized to be discarded and unavailable for subsequent planning. We identify this limitation as the Tool observation bottleneck and propose Latent Visual Evidence-Enhanced Planning (LAVE), a training-free framework for reusing latent visual evidence from completed Tool calls. LAVE introduces a dual-channel observation interface: the visible channel preserves the original textual trajectory, while the latent channel stores pre-verbal visual updates with their Tool roles, source-frame timestamps, and visual locations. During planning, LAVE retrieves evidence relevant to the current Planner state but not covered by textual observations, and integrates it through bounded timestamp-aligned latent updates with entropy-constrained frame-time routing. This enables video agents to reuse existing visual computation without additional training, frame replay, or modifications to the original orchestration. Extensive experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and CG-Bench show that LAVE consistently improves video tool-use agents across backbones. Under a comparable frame budget, LAVE improves the Video-MME overall score by 3.76 points over the strongest baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of latent visual evidence reuse for multi-step video-agent planning.

</details>


### [26] [MAGIC-SSCIL: Manifold Anchoring and Geometric Incremental Calibration for Semi-Supervised Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07586)
*Yousef Abdi,Mohammad Asadpour,Yousef Seyfari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGIC框架，解决无样本的半监督类别增量学习（SSCIL）中特征漂移导致的灾难性遗忘问题。通过软加权几何校准（SWGC）和几何结构对齐（GSA）两个组件，在不存储旧样本的情况下稳定特征空间几何关系。在CIFAR-100、CUB-200和ImageNet-R上以1%、5%、10%标签比例验证，优于多数监督CIL和SSCIL基线，在细粒度低标签设定下增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSCIL在无样本设定下是严峻挑战，现有方法因特征漂移而灾难性遗忘，且随着标签空间增大，伪标签可靠性持续下降，导致模型难以在保持旧知识的同时学习新类别。

Method: MAGIC包含两个核心组件：1) SWGC（软加权几何校准）：在可学习的塑性特征空间上利用基于图的标签传播，对冻结骨干网络计算的类均值和方差进行加权校准，从校准后的高斯分布中采样幻影特征以代表旧任务数据；2) GSA（几何结构对齐）：通过匹配学生和教师头的关系统计结构并让特征锚点与固定分类器原型对齐，锁定特征空间方向，保持表征拓扑结构。

Result: 使用冻结的ResNet-18骨干和可学习塑性适配器实现。在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R上，以1%、5%、10%标签比例验证，MAGIC在平均增量准确率上超越多数配备FixMatch的监督CIL方法和原生SSCIL基线，在细粒度低标签设定中增益最大。

Conclusion: MAGIC通过锚定流形几何和增量校准机制，在不存储样本的情况下有效缓解特征漂移，显著提升SSCIL性能，尤其在伪标签阈值法最失效的细粒度低标签场景下表现突出。

Abstract: Semi-supervised Class Incremental Learning (SSCIL) is a severe challenge for neural networks, and it is hardest in the exemplar-free setting where no past data may be stored. Existing methods forget catastrophically due to feature drift, and their pseudo-labels become increasingly unreliable as the label space grows. In this paper, we propose MAGIC (Manifold Anchoring and Geometric Incremental Calibration), a framework that stabilizes plasticity without storing exemplars. MAGIC's design centers on two components. The first is Soft-Weighted Geometry Calibration (SWGC), which uses graph-based label propagation on the learner's plastic feature space to weight and calibrate class means and variances computed on the frozen backbone; from these calibrated Gaussians, we sample phantom features that stand in for data from previous tasks. The second is a Geometric Structural Alignment (GSA) objective that preserves representation topology by matching the relational structure of student and teacher heads and aligning feature anchors with the fixed classifier prototypes, locking the orientation of the feature space. Together, these constraints keep the adapter from drifting, so geometric relations between classes remain stable as new classes arrive. We implement MAGIC with a frozen ResNet-18 backbone and a learnable plastic adapter. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, and ImageNet-R, at label ratios of 1%, 5%, and 10%, MAGIC improves average incremental accuracy over most of the supervised CIL methods equipped with FixMatch and native SSCIL baselines; the largest gains occur in the fine-grained, low-label setting, where confidence thresholding fails most clearly.

</details>
