MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [87] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自对准复合嵌套晶格模块（共连立方八面体-八面体单元），将几何智能编码于材料系统中，同时满足机器人抓取、自对准、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合需求。通过机械臂和移动装配体组装出家具及建筑尺度结构，压缩测试显示刚度4556 N/mm、最大载荷3445 N、压缩模量17.5 MPa，且模块可拆卸并重用于不同构型，支持可重构循环建造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造系统将材料系统与机器人视为分离的设计问题，智能主要集中于硬件、传感、运动规划和控制。本研究旨在探索如何将几何智能编码在结构化材料系统中，同时满足机器人抓取、自对准、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合等多重需求，从而简化机器人建造对复杂感知和控制算法的依赖。

Method: 设计了一种自对准复合嵌套晶格模块，由共连的立方八面体-八面体单元构成。立方八面体特征提供机器人抓取和对准的确定表面，八面体特征集成可旋松释放的卡扣连接器及相应受体。采用嵌套排列实现沿x、y、z轴的多向互锁聚合。使用机械臂和移动装配体进行组装演示。

Result: 成功演示了家具尺度和建筑尺度的结构组装，包括座椅、跨梁结构、表面和竖向框架。压缩测试得到模块刚度4556 N/mm、最大载荷3445 N、压缩模量17.5 MPa。模块支持拆卸和在不同构型间重用，实现可重构循环建造。

Conclusion: 通过将几何智能编码于材料系统而非仅依赖机器人硬件和算法，该研究证明了结构化模块可以在简化机器人操作需求的同时满足结构性能与可重构性要求，为机器人建造和循环建筑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [88] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出LIRA，一种用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的局部跨层动作条件化机制，将VLM特征到动作解码器的条件化建模为深度感知的信息路由。LIRA通过深度对齐的局部窗口聚合跨层特征，在不改变骨干架构、动作解码器和监督训练流程的情况下，在多个基准（LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D及真实世界操作）上相较VLA-Adapter基线取得显著提升，尤其在零样本迁移到LIBERO-Plus时成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中，VLM中间特征路由到动作解码器的接口未被充分探索。已有设计要么仅暴露表示层级中狭窄的部分，要么将每个解码器块刚性匹配到单个VLM层，限制了跨深度获取互补任务证据的能力，因此需要一种更灵活、深度感知的路由机制。

Method: LIRA提出局部跨层动作条件化机制，将VLM到动作的条件化形式化为深度感知信息路由。它基于任务token特征和从中间VLM状态导出的LIRA Query特征运作，为每个Parallel Fusion Block分配一个以对应VLM层为中心、深度对齐的局部窗口。Parallel Fusion Block聚合邻近的LIRA Query特征，并与任务token特征和本体感觉输入集成后进行动作预测。该路由接口不改变骨干架构、动作解码器和监督训练方案。在0.5B参数配置下与VLA-Adapter基线对比。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D和真实世界操作任务上，LIRA在主要聚合指标上均优于同配置（0.5B参数）的VLA-Adapter基线。在零样本迁移到LIBERO-Plus场景中，平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，获得18.9个百分点的显著增益，表明其在受控分布偏移下具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LIRA通过深度感知的局部跨层路由机制有效改善了VLM特征到动作解码器的信息传递，在多个基准和真实世界任务上取得一致提升，且无需改动模型架构与训练流程，显示了作为VLA接口设计的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [89] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，集成抓取生成、可行性预测与自适应细化，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联的紧凑表示，实现接触感知的抓取位姿生成与触觉引导的动态调整，在仿真和真实环境中显著提升抓取成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺乏接触后的触觉引导自适应机制，导致鲁棒性和泛化性受限。人类能够通过视觉与触觉的无缝集成实现稳定且自适应的抓取，而机器人系统难以复现这一能力，亟需一个统一的视觉-触觉融合框架来解决该问题。

Method: 提出一个统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，包含三大模块：抓取生成、可行性预测和自适应细化。核心创新是引入高效的视觉-触觉表示，通过将触觉信号与手指身份（finger identities）关联，紧密融合物体几何信息与触觉反馈。该统一表示在规划阶段支持接触感知的抓取位姿生成，在接触后支持触觉引导的抓取细化，使系统能够推理细粒度的手指-物体交互并动态调整抓取。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界环境中进行了全面实验，结果表明该方法的抓取成功率显著提升，并且在不同物体的泛化能力得到增强。

Conclusion: 视觉-触觉融合的统一表示框架是提升机器人抓取鲁棒性和泛化性的有效途径，触觉反馈与手指身份的关联编码使得系统能够在接触后动态调整抓取策略，从而更接近人类抓取的表现。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [90] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于扩展现实（XR）的实时6自由度导管追踪和可视化平台，用于患者特异性3D心脏模型中的经皮心脏介入手术。通过结合机器视觉算法（5-DOF）和3D打印机电编码器（测量导管旋转），实现完整6-DOF运动重建。在20名医学生对比试验中，3D可视化组完成任务时间（54.6秒）比传统2D视图（267.5秒）快5倍以上，导管行程减少约5倍（1,939mm vs 7,854mm），同时提高了靶向精度、降低了性能变异，参与者也更青睐3D模式，运动学分析显示3D模式具有更平滑的深度轴导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声技术已有进展，但大多数经皮心脏介入手术仍依赖2D可视化，这限制了操作者的深度感知和空间理解能力，从而影响手术精度和效率。

Method: 开发了一个XR平台，结合自定义机器视觉算法实现5自由度导管追踪，并通过3D打印的机电编码器测量导管横滚角，完整重建6自由度运动。在概念验证研究中，20名新手医学生分别使用沉浸式3D可视化和传统2D导管室视图导航ICE导管到达六个解剖靶点。

Result: 3D组平均任务完成时间为54.6秒（2D组为267.5秒），平均导管行程为1,939mm（2D组为7,854mm），即3D系统快5倍以上、导管行程减少约5倍。3D模式还提高了靶向精度并减少了性能变异。参与者一致认为3D可视化在准确性、速度、可用性和临床价值方面更优。运动学分析显示3D模式下深度轴导航更平滑，而2D用户依赖反复修正动作。

Conclusion: XR-based 3D可视化能够显著提升手术训练效率、精度和运动控制能力，具有改善经皮心脏介入手术训练和临床实践的潜力。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [91] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GWM-VLA，一种基于几何感知潜世界模型的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架。它通过VGGT-Ω聚合多视角观测构建几何感知状态，仅预测目标视角（腕部视角）的patch token，并采用共享潜动作表示同时受潜预测监督和真实机器人动作监督，从而提升VLA模型在视觉与环境变化下的鲁棒性。仿真和真实环境实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在视觉和环境偏移下性能显著下降。潜世界模型虽被视为提升鲁棒性的有前景方案，但已有方法通常独立编码各相机视角并预测整体场景动态，未显式建模视角间的几何关系，导致鲁棒性提升受限。

Method: GWM-VLA包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于VGGT-Ω的几何感知多视角状态编码，在每个时间步联合聚合多视角观测；(2) 全局上下文条件下的目标视角预测，通过多视角聚合后得到的patch token和register token预测目标视角（实验中为腕部视角）的下一时刻patch token，无需预测完整多视角状态；(3) 由机器人动作监督驱动的共享潜动作表示，同时调节潜世界模型与流匹配（flow-matching）动作头，使潜预测监督和真实机器人动作监督共同塑造同一潜动作表示。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界环境中的实验均表明GWM-VLA在机器人操作任务上有效且鲁棒，尤其在视觉和环境偏移条件下相比现有方法表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: GWM-VLA通过显式建模多视角几何关系、精简的目标视角预测策略以及共享潜动作表示，有效提升了VLA模型的鲁棒性和操作性能，验证了几何感知潜世界建模在VLA学习中的价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [92] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRASER，一种具有铰接脊柱的可重构履带式机器人，通过可移动的铰接点和内部质量重定位，利用带簧机构将柔顺性局部化于弯曲区域，同时保持其余车身的高刚度，从而同时提升前端可达性和质心移动能力。实验展示了分别占机器人本体长度74%、66%和59%的台阶、悬空平台和壕沟越障能力，据作者所知为履带式移动机器人中报告的最高水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带式机器人在非结构化环境中广泛使用，但其越障能力从根本上受限于前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的权衡。现有设计难以同时优化这两个相互矛盾的指标，需要一种既能延伸前端到达更远障碍物又保持重心稳定性的新机构。

Method: 提出TRASER履带式机器人，采用带簧（tape-spring）机构将柔顺性局部化在弯曲区域，同时保持车身其余部分的高刚度。机器人能够同时重定位铰接点和内部质量（质心）。基于几何模型和静力学模型分析铰接点位置和质心位置对台阶及壕沟越障性能的影响。

Result: 实验验证了TRASER分别实现占车身长度74%的台阶越障、66%的悬空平台越障以及59%的壕沟越障。据作者所知，这些结果代表了履带式移动机器人中最高的越障能力报告。

Conclusion: 通过将铰接点重定位与内部质量重分配相结合，TRASER有效突破了传统履带式机器人在前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的固有权衡，显著提升了复杂地形下的越障能力。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [93] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hölder signed distance的新颖可微凸多面体间有符号距离计算方法。通过引入可微的Hölder最小值和最大值算子替代经典SDF公式中的min-max算子，该距离可用闭式表达计算，避免了迭代算法的收敛问题，同时保持可微性和符号信息，并天然适合GPU并行化。实验验证了其计算性能和机械臂控制应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动规划与控制中，集合间距离计算至关重要，需要可微梯度以实现实时优化。然而欧几里得有符号距离函数（SDF）并非处处可微，现有替代方法往往在可微性、符号信息和计算效率之间存在权衡取舍。

Method: 首先提出Hölder最小值和Hölder最大值算子作为传统min-max算子的可微版本，然后将它们替换经典SDF公式中的原始min-max算子，从而得到Hölder signed distance。该方法以闭式解形式计算，不需要迭代算法。

Result: 通过运行时比较验证了该方法相比现有方法的计算优势，并通过机械臂操作实验展示了其在控制应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该闭式可微有符号距离方法消除了迭代算法的收敛问题，同时保持可微性和符号信息，具备计算效率高、适合GPU并行化的优点，适用于机器人控制等实时应用场景。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [94] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对自动化仓库中高密度存储与高吞吐检索的权衡问题，提出了一种新颖的多机器人有序存储/检索问题(OSRP)求解方法。该方法利用"免重排摆放"(relocation-free arrangement)的几何性质，设计了一个在线的、基于优先级的、对存储布局不变量加以利用的多智能体寻径(MAPF)算法，保证完备性并防止死锁，实现了可扩展性。实验表明，在机器人数量不超过网格宽度C时，makespan随机器人数量近似线性改善，且支持鲁棒性(应对离场序列不确定性)时算法开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化仓库在最大化存储密度与实现高检索吞吐量之间面临根本性权衡。PBS(类拼图存储)架构通过取消通道来提高容量，但在这种高密度空间中协调多个机器人极易产生死锁，计算上极具挑战。现有集中式规划方法难以扩展到大规模场景，亟需一种既能保证几何可行性又能实现高效执行的多机器人协调方法。

Method: 本文提出一种新的多机器人形式化模型，针对"最大容量下的有序存储与检索问题"，聚焦于可从单一边界访问的矩形网格。核心方法利用免重排摆放的性质来弥合几何可行性与执行效率之间的差距，据此设计了一个在线的、基于优先级的多智能体寻径算法。该算法不同于通用集中式规划器，它利用存储布局的特定不变量来保证完备性并防止死锁，从而实现可扩展性；此外还引入鲁棒存储布局以应对离场序列的不确定性。

Result: 实验表明，在机器人数量m从1到C(C为网格宽度)的范围内，该方法的makespan(完工时间)随机器人数量呈近似线性改善。更重要的是，支持鲁棒性的算法开销可忽略不计——相比非鲁棒基线，采用鲁棒存储布局应对离场序列不确定性时，执行速度没有显著损失。

Conclusion: 本文证明，通过深入挖掘特定存储布局的几何不变量，可以设计出既有完备性保证又具有良好可扩展性的高效多机器人协调算法，从而在PBS架构中同时实现高存储密度和高检索吞吐。此外，鲁棒性(a)与执行效率并不矛盾，可以通过巧妙的布局设计以几乎为零的代价实现。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [95] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回旋曲线（clothoid/Euler螺旋）的自适应前视距离横向控制策略，结合准反馈控制算法和前馈滤波器，通过TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真验证，显著提升了自动驾驶车辆的横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统横向控制方法在曲率突变时易导致乘客不适和车辆侧翻风险，且固定前视距离难以适应多变的道路几何与车辆动力学条件。作者旨在通过平滑曲率过渡和自适应前视机制来解决这些问题。

Method: 采用基于欧拉螺旋（clothoid）的轨迹构建方法实现平滑曲率过渡；引入根据实时车辆动力学和道路几何自适应调整的前视距离机制；在每个时间步通过准反馈控制算法构建最优clothoid以生成转向指令；利用前馈滤波器补偿车辆横向动力学滞后，提升控制响应与稳定性。

Result: 通过TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真，在多种驾驶场景下验证了该控制器的有效性，结果表明相比传统方法在横向控制性能上有显著提升，包括横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度的改善。

Conclusion: 基于clothoid的自适应前视横向控制策略能有效兼顾路径跟踪精度与乘坐舒适性，未来可通过将控制器扩展到更高车速场景并优化离轨误差，进一步适配铰接式车辆等复杂应用。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [96] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoCoNav是一个人群导航框架，结合在线共形校准与运行时认证的规划方法，通过水平特定的共形比例-积分控制器自适应轨迹误差界限，并采用"松弛-验证"规划器在保持求解器可行性的同时认证轨迹安全，在仿真和四足机器人实验中取得了碰撞避免、任务成功率和导航效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式方法在人群导航中可能产生振荡行为，而预测性规划器往往将预测视为精确或依赖限制性的误差模型；将保守不确定性集作为硬约束会使模型预测控制（MPC）不可行。因此需要一种既能处理预测误差不确定性和漂移，又能保持规划可行性的导航框架。

Method: 提出CoCoNav框架：(1) 在线共形校准模块，采用水平特定的共形比例-积分（PI）控制器调节轨迹误差界，以控制长时经验覆盖率并适应变化的预测误差；(2) "松弛-验证"（relax-then-verify）规划器，先用软约束MPC生成名义轨迹以保持求解器可行性，再结合应急机动（contingency maneuvers）对轨迹进行运行时认证，确保其满足校准后的误差界限。

Result: 在仿真和四足机器人（quadruped）实验中，CoCoNav相比评估的基线方法，在碰撞避免、任务成功率和导航效率之间实现了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: 通过解耦"生成"与"验证"两个阶段，CoCoNav有效解决了保守不确定性约束导致MPC不可行的问题，同时利用在线共形校准使框架能够动态适应预测误差漂移，为不确定环境下的安全人群导航提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高海拔积雪地形、由无人机(UAV)与地面无人车(UGV)协同的导航框架。该系统采用定制的高效U-Net网络进行实时道路分割（精度96.5%），结合合成雪地数据增强技术；通过EKF融合GPS/IMU实现无人机定位（最大误差±0.5米）；利用YOLOv5与RGB-D深度数据跟踪定位UGV；并基于动态路径规划算法适应雪堆变化，实现遮蔽环境下的可靠导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高海拔积雪地形中，能见度低且地面不稳定，传统导航方法失效，亟需一种融合空中与地面平台能力的协同导航方案。

Method: 1) 设计高效U-Net架构用于实时道路分割，并采用新颖的合成雪数据增强技术提升性能；2) 使用扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）融合GPS与IMU数据进行UAV定位；3) 通过YOLOv5目标检测配合UAV的RGB-D相机深度数据实现UGV视觉跟踪定位；4) 利用分割结果驱动动态路径规划，自适应调整以应对雪堆漂移。

Result: 道路分割精度达到96.5%；UAV定位最大位置误差为±0.5米；在遮蔽测试环境中实现了成功导航，偏差极小。

Conclusion: 该协同一体化框架有效克服了高海拔积雪环境的挑战，验证了深度学习方法与经典滤波、路径规划算法相结合在极端地形导航中的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [98] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人抓取硬币等薄平物体的难题，通过制造中心Shore E10硅胶、两侧Shore A60的混合刚度仿人指尖，并与均匀软E10指尖对比，在自动斜向旋转指尖捏取实验中，验证了在"指甲"支撑的软垫内，通过材料选择而非解剖结构创建额外约束边界，可扩展几何约束成立的操作条件范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取薄而平的物体（如桌面硬币）仍具挑战性，传统方法需重新定向物体、接触物体底面或添加专用指甲机构。此前已证明刚性"指甲"能止住软垫变形并形成几何约束以提高抓取精度，但作者想知道是否能在垫内通过材料选择创建额外约束边界，从而在不同的软硬配置下扩展该约束成立的条件。

Method: 制作中心Shore E10、两侧Shore A60的混合刚度仿人指尖，与均匀E10软指尖对比。用自动化装置进行斜向旋转指尖捏取，使垫接触硬币侧面并将其从无缝隙的平贴状态抬起。在水平接近距离、垂直手指位移和食指旋转角度等参数变化下，比较两种指尖在几何约束形成及过渡到稳定抓取阶段的表现。

Result: 混合刚度指尖在更多测试设置下保持高成功率；无指甲指尖在所有36个条件中全部失败。但在沿手指轴向变化硬币位置时，均匀指尖表现更好，存在条件依赖的权衡。经硬币尺寸调参后，两种指尖都能抓取全部六种日本硬币面值。

Conclusion: 薄物体抓取的工作范围不仅取决于垫的柔软度，还取决于刚度在指甲支撑垫内的放置位置。边界放置位置可被视为指尖设计的有用变量，这为优化薄物体抓取性能提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [99] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人演示数据集中"指令-轨迹不匹配"（ITM）问题的后验审计。提出MMPF——一种无需训练的审计框架，将每个模态视为专家，通过局部邻域一致性与全局原型相似度估计任务标签分布，并以预测熵加权进行专家融合。在LIBERO基准与真实机器人数据上取得了最优的ITM检测与标签纠正精度，并验证了审计能改善下游策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉-语言-动作策略训练所用的演示数据集中，存在一种隐蔽且有害的失败模式：轨迹行为正确但配错了语言指令。这类ITM与失败轨迹不同，往往看似合理，会破坏策略学到的语言-行为映射关系，亟需有效的后验审计方法。

Method: 提出MMPF（Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion）审计框架：1）将每个模态视为一个专家；2）通过局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似度分别估计任务标签分布；3）采用预测熵加权、以专家乘积（product of experts）方式融合各模态的标签估计。

Result: 在LIBERO基准（含注入的指令不匹配）和含噪真实机器人数据上，MMPF取得了最强的整体ITM检测与标签纠正精度；在需要语言消歧任务的场景中，审计能改善多数下游策略学习；真实机器人实验验证了方法可提升策略性能，并展示了过滤演示与重新标注的权衡。

Conclusion: MMPF作为一种训练免费的审计框架，能有效检测和纠正指令-轨迹不匹配问题，并可通过过滤或重标签两种方式提升下游策略性能。在需要语言消歧的任务中表现出明显优势。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [100] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-WAM，一种与模型无关的训练策略，通过表征对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界动作模型（WAMs），利用运动对齐和目标对齐两个互补目标，使WAMs学习轨迹级别的时空表征，提升空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在2D像素空间中表示视频，与机器人执行动作的3D空间之间存在表征鸿沟。虽然最近的3D方法引入了3D信息，但未能充分利用3D结构的动力学特性，缺乏对轨迹级时空表征的学习。

Method: 提出模型无关的训练策略4D-WAM，通过表征对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入WAMs。包含两个目标：1）运动对齐——对齐相邻帧之间的时间特征变化，鼓励模型在训练中建立局部4D感知；2）目标对齐——通过最小化注意力相似性分布之间的差距，引导模型从源帧推断最终目的地。两个目标分别提供局部运动监督和长时程目标引导。

Result: 在多种基础模型上的分布内和分布外实验中，4D-WAM在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化能力和通用性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过局部运动监督与长时程目标引导的结合，4D-WAM能够使WAMs学习轨迹级别的时空表征，有效弥合2D视觉表征与3D动作空间之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>


### [101] [DA-NBV: A Direction-Aware Next-Best-View Planner for Efficient 3D Reconstruction of Ships at Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08025)
*Jiaming Chen,Juntao Yang,Zhentao Zou,Qi Ming,Yi Yu,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang,Xue Jiang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-NBV，一种方向感知的下一最佳视角（NBV）规划策略，用于海上船舶的三维重建。该方法在传统占据状态基础上补充方向观测统计信息，引入可学习的位姿优势场（PAF）引导视角选择，并采用局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖塑造奖励来提升扫描效率，同时开发了SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置海况仿真环境，实验证明其在重建完整性和精度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上船舶的精确三维重建对海事监管、损害评估和自主海上作业至关重要。现有3D重建技术主要依赖人工设计轨迹或专业操作员，成本高且可扩展性差。现有的NBV策略主要建模空间占据而忽略方向性观测历史，这尤其不利于船舶这类具有复杂上层结构和严重自遮挡的目标——需要从多个视点观测，而方向覆盖不足常导致重建不完整。此外，海上的波浪起伏（垂荡、横摇、纵摇）持续改变船舶姿态和表面可见性，风力干扰和有限的载板功率也对扫描效率提出更高要求。

Method: DA-NBV框架包含以下核心组件：(1) 方向感知状态表示——在传统占据状态基础上增补方向观测统计信息；(2) 可学习的位姿优势场（PAF）——利用方向信息引导视角选择；(3) 局部约束动作空间——限制视角搜索范围以提高规划效率；(4) 非线性覆盖塑造奖励——设计非线性奖励函数引导策略优化扫描覆盖；同时构建了面向船舶的SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置的海况仿真环境以支持训练和评估。

Result: 在不同垂荡、横摇和纵摇条件下，DA-NBV相比现有方法将重建完整度提升约3个百分点，将Chamfer距离降低43%，同时实现了更高的路径效率。实验在可配置海况仿真环境中进行，验证了方法在动态海况下的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: DA-NBV通过引入方向感知机制有效解决了海上船舶因自遮挡和波浪扰动导致的重建不完整问题，在重建质量、精度和路径效率方面均取得了显著改进，为海上自主三维重建提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of ships at sea is important for maritime supervision, damage assessment, and autonomous maritime operations. Although 3D reconstruction has advanced considerably, high-quality data acquisition still largely relies on manually designed trajectories or skilled operators, resulting in high costs and limited scalability. Next-best-view (NBV) planning automates this process by selecting subsequent viewpoints based on the current state. However, existing NBV policies mainly model spatial occupancy while overlooking directional observation history. This limitation is particularly problematic for ships: their complex superstructures and severe self-occlusions require observations from multiple viewpoints, and insufficient directional coverage often yields incomplete reconstructions. These challenges are further amplified at sea, where wave-induced heave, roll, and pitch continuously alter the ship's pose and surface visibility. Meanwhile, wind disturbances and limited onboard power impose stricter requirements on scanning efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose DA-NBV, a direction-aware NBV policy that augments the conventional occupancy state with directional observation statistics. We introduce a learnable Position Advantage Field (PAF) that uses directional information to guide viewpoint selection. The policy further adopts a locally constrained action space and a nonlinear coverage-shaping reward to improve scanning efficiency. We also develop the ship-oriented SeaShip-3D dataset and a configurable sea-state simulation environment. Experiments under varying heave, roll, and pitch conditions show that DA-NBV improves reconstruction completeness by approximately 3 percentage points and reduces Chamfer distance by 43% while achieving higher path efficiency.

</details>


### [102] [Compiling and Benchmarking Task-State Horizons for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08036)
*Meiqi Wang,Shichao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务状态地平线（TSH）这一新指标来量化智能体在长时间跨度任务中需要跟踪的状态转换范围，并发布了RoboGraph任务编译器及包含588个场景片段的基准测试，在语义和视觉闭环环境中评估了15种先进智能体模型，发现大多数模型在高TSH任务中表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要通过动作序列长度和子任务复杂度来刻画任务难度，忽略了智能体必须跟踪由自身探索和环境动态共同引起的演化中任务相关世界状态这一独特挑战。需要一个能够量化并评估智能体跟踪长跨度状态转换能力的新视角。

Method: 定义了任务状态地平线（TSH）作为度量指标，构建了RoboGraph任务编译器，将状态转换依赖关系转化为可执行的符号图。RoboGraph从空间和时间因果依赖中构建任务状态地平线，包括任务执行中由意外故障和干预引起的依赖。在此基础上构建了包含84个场景、588个片段的基准测试，并在语义和视觉两种闭环环境中评估了15种先进智能体模型。

Result: 实验表明大多数先进智能体模型在面对高任务状态地平线时表现挣扎，在维护、探索和更新长时程任务相关状态方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 现有前沿智能体模型在长时程状态跟踪能力上仍存在明显不足，任务状态地平线是区分任务难度的核心维度，需进一步推动智能体在长时程任务中维护和更新世界状态能力的发展。

Abstract: Frontier agentic models are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for long-horizon embodied tasks. Existing robotic benchmarks have advanced long-horizon evaluation, but primarily characterize difficulty through action-sequence length and subtask complexity, overlooking a distinct challenge: agents must track evolving task-relevant world states induced by both their exploration and environmental dynamics. We define the span of task-relevant state transitions that an agent must track as task-state horizon (TSH). To evaluate how agent performance varies with TSH, we introduce RoboGraph, a robotic task compiler that translates state-transition dependencies into executable symbolic graphs. Specifically, RoboGraph constructs task-state horizons from spatial and temporal causal dependencies, including those induced by unexpected failures and interventions during task execution. Building on RoboGraph, we release a benchmark comprising 588 episodes across 84 scenes with varying TSHs. Experiments evaluating 15 advanced agentic models in both semantic and visual closed-loop environments show that most models struggle with demanding TSHs, revealing substantial gaps in maintaining, exploring, and updating task-relevant state over long horizon.

</details>


### [103] [PhysX-CoT: Structured Physical Reasoning from a Single Image to Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08053)
*Jie Huang,Xiaohe Li,Jiahao Li,Fangli Mou,Chen Qian,Yuqiang Fang,Junhao Fan,Kaixin Zhang,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysX-CoT将单图像3D资产生成建模为显式的结构化物理推理过程（可机器解析的部件级状态轨迹），通过解耦几何（3D框负责放置、局部编码负责形状）与CoT对齐的GRPO优化（覆盖解析有效性、接地、几何、放置和物理一致性），在统一协议下超越最接近的全任务基线，并验证显式状态的功能价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D资产生成采用输出中心式框架（VLM输出序列化资产），图像到3D的推理是隐式的。限制因素在于：部件放置和局部形状在单一全局坐标token流中纠缠，中间物理状态从未暴露用于监督、条件化或验证。

Method: 将资产生成转化为显式的结构化物理推理：有序、可机器解析的部件级状态轨迹（分解、2D/3D接地、关系、粗几何、表面线索），各部分分别监督、用于几何条件化并作为奖励目标。几何被分解为3D框（放置）+局部编码（形状），采用CoT对齐的GRPO优化解析有效性、接地、几何、放置和物理一致性。

Result: 在统一协议（相同主干、数据和冻结解码器）下，PhysX-CoT在几何、尺度和物理属性指标上均超越最接近的全任务基线；Oracle、token匹配和状态顺序控制实验表明显式状态是功能性的而非装饰性的；在UE5中生成的资产以高有效性完成解析、碰撞和关节化。

Conclusion: 显式的中间物理状态（部件级状态轨迹）具备真正的功能价值，可分别监督、条件化几何并作为奖励目标，显著提升单图像3D资产生成的性能与下游实用性（如UE5中的应用）。

Abstract: Simulation-ready 3D assets are central to robotics and embodied AI. Generating them from a single image is usually framed as a vision-language model that emits a serialized asset for a decoder to turn into geometry and physical fields, leaving the image-to-3D reasoning implicit. We argue the limiting factor is this output-centric view: part placement and local shape are entangled in one global-coordinate token stream, and the intermediate physical states are never exposed for supervision, conditioning, or verification. PhysX-CoT instead casts single-image asset generation as an explicit structured physical reasoning process, an ordered and machine-parseable trajectory of part-level states covering decomposition, 2D and 3D grounding, relations, coarse geometry, and surface cues that we separately supervise, use to condition geometry, and treat as reward targets. Geometry is factorized so that 3D boxes carry placement and local codes carry shape, and CoT-aligned GRPO optimizes parse validity, grounding, geometry, placement, and physical consistency. Under a unified protocol that retrains all learned baselines on the same backbone, data, and frozen decoder, PhysX-CoT outperforms the closest full-task baseline across geometry, scale, and physical-attribute metrics. Oracle, token-matched, and state-order controls show the explicit states are functional rather than cosmetic, and in Unreal Engine~5 the generated assets parse, collide, and articulate at high validity.

</details>


### [104] [SurgWMBench: A Vision-Based Benchmark for World-Modeling Surgical Instrument Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08070)
*Huanrong Liu,Weiliang Huang,Bob Zhang,Weichao Cai,Chunlin Tian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgWMBench，一个基于视觉的短期手术运动规划与动力学预测基准，用于评估手术世界模型在预测近未来器械运动以及连续展开或输入扰动下稳定性方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法缺乏对器械运动建模的能力，而工具运动预测方法又不能捕捉未来手术视频状态的耦合演化。此外，现有手术世界模型研究过度依赖生成质量指标（如FVD、CD-FVD），这些指标与器械运动规划不对齐，且领域缺乏公开数据集和标准化评估协议。

Method: 提出了SurgWMBench基准，它利用术中图像序列和历史器械轨迹，同时评估近未来器械运动预测以及模型在连续滚动推理或输入扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 论文尚未给出具体实验结果，主要贡献在于定义了一个运动导向的手术世界模型评估基准和标准化的基准测试基础设施。

Conclusion: SurgWMBench为手术世界模型的运动中心能力提供了标准化评估框架，弥合了视觉生成质量与器械运动规划之间的评估鸿沟，为可靠的手术规划提供了基础支撑。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models that move beyond recognizing the current surgical step or imitating expert demonstrations, and instead anticipate how instrument motion reshapes subsequent operative states. Most surgical video understanding methods focus on recognizing phases, actions, or workflow states, while providing limited support for explicitly modeling instrument motion. Conversely, existing tool motion prediction methods can forecast instrument trajectories, but they generally do not capture the coupled evolution of future surgical video states. World models offer a natural framework for jointly modeling visual state transitions and instrument motion dynamics. However, existing surgical world model studies remain largely centered on visual generation quality, relying on generation-oriented metrics such as FVD and CD-FVD. These metrics are poorly aligned with instrument motion planning, as they do not directly measure whether predicted trajectories are geometrically accurate, temporally coherent, or actionable for downstream planning. This limitation is partly structural, since the field lacks public datasets and standardized evaluation protocols that provide the benchmarking infrastructure needed to assess motion-centric capabilities in surgical world models. In this paper, we introduce SurgWMBench, a vision-based benchmark for short-horizon surgical motion planning and dynamics prediction. Given intraoperative image sequences and historical instrument trajectory, SurgWMBench evaluates both near-future instrument motion prediction and stability under continuous rollout or input perturbations.

</details>


### [105] [Event-Time Hybrid Optimal Control for Robotic Table Tennis Serves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08157)
*Thomas Gossard,Till Köpff,Andreas Ziegler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件时间最优控制（OCP）的机器人乒乓球发球生成方法，通过优化球拍击球速度、朝向以及弹跳、过网、落地等关键时间节点，在相边界直接施加桌面反弹和过网约束，并将球拍运动转化为满足关节位置、速度与力矩限制的完整运动轨迹。数值与KUKA Agilus机器人实验表明，相比固定步长方法，解算时间中位数降低4.1倍，实际落点误差均值13.1±7.3 cm，可生成含上旋、下旋、侧旋的受控发球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人乒乓球发球需要高球速与高旋转，同时须满足机器人运动学与动力学限制。与对拉不同，发球必须先在己方台面反弹并过网，构成具有非线性飞行和撞击动力学的混合系统，传统固定步长方法在相边界约束处理与求解效率上存在不足。

Method: 将带旋转控制的发球生成建模为事件时间OCP，联合优化球拍击球速度与朝向，以及反弹、过网、落地三个事件时间，使台面反弹和过网约束可直接在相边界上强制实施。随后通过第二个OCP将球拍速度与朝向转换为满足关节位置、速度和力矩限制的完整运动轨迹。

Result: 数值实验与KUKA Agilus真机实验验证，事件时间公式相比固定步长加根定位方法，中位数求解时间减少4.1倍，同时保持相当的落点精度、旋转精度与发球有效性。真机实验实现受控落点及上旋、下旋、侧旋发球，平均落点误差13.1±7.3 cm，旋转速率可达30 rps。

Conclusion: 事件时间最优控制能够在考虑非线性空气动力与撞击效应的同时，高效生成物理可行、运动学动力学可行的发球，为机器人乒乓球发球控制提供了有效的求解框架。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis serves require high ball velocity and spin while respecting the robot's kinodynamic limits. Unlike rally strokes, a valid serve must also bounce on the server's side and clear the net, yielding a hybrid system with nonlinear flight and impact dynamics. We formulate spin-controlled serve generation as an event-time \ac{OCP} that optimizes the racket impact velocity and orientation together with the bounce, net-crossing, and landing times, enabling direct enforcement at phase boundaries of table-bounce and net-clearance constraints. The racket velocity and orientation are then converted into a complete kinodynamically feasible motion through a second \ac{OCP} enforcing joint-position, velocity, and torque limits. We evaluate the method numerically and on a KUKA Agilus robot. Compared with a fixed-step formulation with root localization, the proposed event-time formulation reduces the median solve time by a factor of 4.1 while maintaining comparable landing accuracy, spin accuracy, and serve validity. Real-robot experiments demonstrate controlled placement and topspin, backspin, and sidespin serves, with a mean landing error of $13.1 \pm 7.3$~cm and spin rates up to 30~rps. These results show that event-time optimal control efficiently generates physically valid, kinodynamically feasible serves while accounting for nonlinear aerodynamic and impact effects.

</details>


### [106] [Multi-modal Interactive Control of Robotic Arm based on Offline Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08183)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了"Socratic Models-ChatGLM"算法，基于离线开源大语言模型ChatGLM，在PyBullet仿真平台上实现机械臂的多模态交互控制，能够解决复杂的图文集成多步长时距机械臂操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数主流大语言模型（如ChatGPT）并非友好开源且需要持续付费，成本高昂。因此，在本地服务器上部署开源大语言模型被视为更低成本、更稳定免费使用的有效途径，以此支持创意具身AI算法的设计与实现。

Method: 基于Socratic Models框架与开源大语言模型ChatGLM相结合，构建了"Socratic Models-ChatGLM"算法。该算法通过离线大语言模型在PyBullet仿真平台实现机械臂的多模态交互控制，利用图文融合机制处理多模态输入并完成长时距任务规划与执行。

Result: 该方法在多模态交互控制的机械臂任务中表现出良好性能，并展现出解决复杂的文本-图像集成多步长时距机械臂操作任务的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 将开源大语言模型与多模态交互控制框架结合，可以低成本、稳定地实现复杂的长时距机械臂操作任务，为具身AI算法的落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly revolutionized the modern society with numerous advanced interactions between humans and AI agents, whereas the usage of most large language models including ChatGPT are not friendly open-sourced and must require the users paying a lot for such AI services continuously. Therefore, deploying open-sourced large language models on local servers can be considered as an efficient approach to design and implement creative embodied AI algorithms with lower cost and more stable free usage. Inspired by this ordinary motivation, we originally propose and implement the "Socratic Models-ChatGLM", which is a well-performed algorithm for multi-modal interactive control of robotic arm based on offline large language models via the facile PyBullet platform, even presents extraordinary potential to address complicated text-image integrated multi-step long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [107] [Spatiotemporal Context-dependent Personalized Movement Compensation in Delayed Telemanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08200)
*Sai Jiang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以人为中心的个性化运动缩放方法，为每位参与者拟合延迟、方向和距离特异的缩放参数，以缓解遥操作中的通信延迟问题。在虚拟模拟器和遥手术机器人上评估表明，运动缩放可带来高达20-25%的性能提升，且延迟越长效果越显著，个性化在特定条件下可提供额外精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信延迟是遥操作中的核心挑战之一，会破坏视觉运动协调并降低任务精度。运动缩放虽是应对延迟引起的过冲的有效手段，但传统部署采用统一增益，忽略了参与者的个体差异和任务情境（方向、距离、延迟）的变化，缺乏个性化与自适应能力。

Method: 提出一种拟合每个参与者的延迟、方向和距离特异性缩放参数的个性化方法。20名参与者在虚拟模拟器中执行延迟到达任务，在每种实验条件组合下通过仿真计算最小化平均过冲的缩放增益，并在仿真及遥手术机器人上进行评估验证。

Result: 运动缩放相对于无辅助条件持续改善性能，关键指标提升达20-25%，在较长延迟下效果最为显著。个性化在中等延迟、短距离的内向到达任务中展现了额外的精度收益。评估涵盖过冲量、端点误差、轨迹平滑度、运动经济性及综合误差-时间指标等多个维度。

Conclusion: 个性化缩放可作为更自适应遥操作框架的基础，通过整合情境信息提升远程手术等程序的安全性与精度，具备推广到更复杂、变化场景的潜力。

Abstract: Communication delay remains a central challenge in telerobotics, where it disrupts visuomotor coordination and reduces task precision. Motion scaling is an effective countermeasure to delay-induced overshoot, yet typical deployments rely on uniform gains that neglect individual and contextual variability. We propose a human-centered method that fits personalized delay-, direction-, and distance-specific scaling parameters for each participant. We conducted experiments with twenty participants who performed delayed reaching tasks in a virtual simulator. Scaling gains were computed to minimize mean overshoot in simulation in each combination of experimental conditions. Evaluation was done in simulation and on a telesurgical robot to evaluate assistance benefits. Performance was assessed across multiple delays, distances, and movement directions using overshoot, endpoint error, trajectory smoothness, economy of motion, and a composite error-time metric. Motion scaling consistently improved performance relative to unassisted trials, yielding up to 20-25% performance gains in key metrics. Effects were most pronounced at longer delays. Personalization demonstrated additional accuracy benefits for inward reaching at a short distance under moderate delay. The results highlight the potential of personalized scaling as a foundation for more adaptive frameworks that integrate contextual information to improve the safety and precision of teleoperated procedures.

</details>


### [108] [Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08273)
*Hwanhee Kim,Jaehyun Jang,Seungmin Cha,Hyeonseo Yun,Donghoon Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ALVA（Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment），一种面向视觉具身智能体的轨迹级评估器。ALVA通过两阶段预训练VLM处理：先基于执行动作序列对帧间视觉转换进行摘要，再结合自然语言指令评估摘要生成离散的轨迹级进度分数。在模拟3D家庭环境中，ALVA展现出保守的评估模式，近零假阳性率，用作闭环策略优化的终末反馈时优于静态图像和基于嵌入的视觉基线，并缩小了与真实标注oracle的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉具身智能体执行多步自然语言指令时需要反馈机制来评估整个轨迹的任务进展。传统方法基于最终帧匹配或连续嵌入相似度，可能忽略判断指令是否完成所必需的中间转换信息，因此需要更全面的轨迹级评估方法。

Method: ALVA采用预训练VLM的两阶段框架：(1) 摘要阶段：基于执行的动作序列对帧间视觉转换进行条件化总结，捕捉中间状态变化；(2) 评估阶段：将生成的摘要与自然语言指令进行比对，输出离散的轨迹级进度分数。该方法同时以视觉观测、执行的动作序列和自然语言指令为条件进行整体评估。

Result: 在模拟3D家庭环境中，ALVA表现出保守评估模式，具有近零假阳性率。作为闭环策略优化的终末反馈时，ALVA提供了比静态图像和基于嵌入的视觉基线更有效的反馈，并减少了与ground-truth oracle之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 动作与语言条件化的视频评估（ALVA）可作为所评估模拟具身控制任务中一种可解释的反馈机制，捕捉了传统方法忽略的轨迹中间转换信息，从而更准确地评估多步指令的完成情况。

Abstract: Vision-based embodied agents executing multi-step natural language instructions require feedback mechanisms that assess task progress over complete trajectories. Conventional approaches based on final-frame matching or continuous embedding similarity may overlook intermediate transitions that are necessary for determining whether an instruction has been completed. We propose ALVA (Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment), a trajectory evaluator that conditions its assessment on visual observations, the executed action sequence, and the natural language instruction. The method uses a pre-trained vision-language model (VLM) in two stages: it first summarizes frame-to-frame visual transitions conditioned on the executed actions and then assesses the generated summary with respect to the instruction to produce a discrete trajectory-level progress score. In simulated 3D household environments, ALVA exhibits a conservative assessment pattern with near-zero false-positive rates. When used as terminal feedback for closed-loop policy optimization, it provides more effective feedback than the evaluated static image and embedding-based visual baselines and reduces the performance gap to a ground-truth oracle. These results support action- and language-conditioned video assessment as an interpretable feedback mechanism for the evaluated simulated embodied-control tasks.

</details>


### [109] [Scalable High-Speed Lateral Control for Single-Body and Articulated Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08293)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的横向控制框架，用于单车体和铰接式自动驾驶车辆的高速路径跟踪。基于回旋曲线控制器进行了三项关键改进：集成切向检查与弗雷歇距离方法的优化前瞻距离及振荡抑制、实时轨迹段分类以动态调整前瞻搜索范围、以及响应横向误差和前方路径几何的双自适应速率控制前瞻机制。通过高保真TruckSim和Simulink仿真验证，在双车道变换和蜿蜒道路场景下实现了稳定高速跟踪、低横向误差和可接受的侧向加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速场景下自动驾驶车辆（尤其是铰接式车辆）的路径跟踪存在振荡、前瞻距离选择困难以及不同车辆构型（单车体vs铰接式）需要统一控制架构的问题，亟需一个可扩展且鲁棒的横向控制框架。

Method: 将回旋曲线（clothoid）控制器扩展三项关键自适应机制：(1) 集成切向检查和弗雷歇距离方法，优化前瞻距离选择并抑制振荡；(2) 利用实时轨迹段分类动态调整前瞻搜索范围；(3) 采用双自适应、速率控制的前瞻机制，响应横向误差和前方路径几何。同时引入灵活的跟踪点选择和曲率-转向查找表，支持拖拉机后轴、挂接点或挂车相关位置的控制而无需改变核心控制架构。

Result: 在高保真TruckSim和Simulink仿真中，对单车体和铰接式车辆在双车道变换和蜿蜒道路场景下进行测试，结果表明：高速路径跟踪稳定、振荡显著减少、前瞻距离自适应有效、横向误差低、侧向加速度可接受，且适用于不同车辆配置。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了统一横向控制架构从单车体扩展到铰接式自动驾驶车辆的可行性，为高速场景下的多种车辆构型提供了一种鲁棒且可扩展的路径跟踪解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable lateral control framework for robust path tracking of single-body and articulated autonomous vehicles at high speeds. A clothoid-based controller is extended with three key adaptations: 1) an integrated tangential check and Frechet distance method for optimized lookahead and oscillation mitigation; 2) real-time trajectory segment classification for dynamic adjustment of the lookahead search range; and 3) a dual-adaptive, rate-controlled lookahead mechanism responsive to cross-track error and upcoming path geometry. To support different vehicle configurations, the framework also incorporates flexible tracking point selection and curvature-to-steering lookup tables, enabling control of points such as the tractor rear axle, hitch point, or trailer-related locations without changing the core control architecture. The controller is evaluated using high-fidelity TruckSim and Simulink simulations for both standalone and articulated vehicles across dual lane changes and winding-road scenarios. Results demonstrate stable high-speed path tracking, reduced oscillations, effective lookahead adaptation, low cross-track errors, and acceptable lateral acceleration across different vehicle configurations. The results support the use of a unified lateral control architecture that can scale from single-body to articulated autonomous vehicles.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张将（计算）论证形式化作为评估性AI（EAI）系统的计算基础，支持可解释、可质疑的人类决策辅助，为分布式、以人为中心的EAI研究议程奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估性AI（EAI）提出通过呈现竞争性假设及其正反证据来支持人类决策，而非给出单一推荐。作者认为需要一种正式、可计算的基础来支撑EAI的可解释性和可质疑性，而计算论证恰是满足这一需求的合适范式。

Method: 本文为立场论文，论证性地阐明计算论证形式化与EAI之间的适配关系，提出以计算论证作为EAI的形式化基础，并勾勒了面向分布式与以人为中心的EAI系统的长期研究议程。

Result: 论文提出并论证了计算论证是EAI可解释、可质疑形式化基础的合适范式，为未来研究设定了方向性框架。

Conclusion: 计算论证为EAI提供了正式且可计算的基础支撑，是实现可解释、可质疑、分布式及以人为中心的EAI系统的关键路径，值得作为长期研究议程推进。

Abstract: Evaluative AI (EAI) has been recently proposed as a way to support human decision-making, not by producing a single recommendation, but by presenting competing hypotheses together with evidence for and against each. In this position paper, we advocate (computational) argumentation as a particularly suitable paradigm to provide a formal, computable foundation for forms of EAI that are explainable and contestable, setting the ground for a long-term research agenda towards distributed and human-centred EAI systems.

</details>


### [111] [Flow-by-Flow:Content-Judgment Bypass for Governing AI Output in High-Loss Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07474)
*Hiroki Naito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"逐流治理"（Flow-by-Flow）范式，指出人类监督在高损失领域中因AI输出速度与认知负荷的乘积（V×L）超过人类认知容量C_max而结构性崩溃。论文论证AI能力提升只会重构而非减少认知负荷，并提出基于形式化可计数特征的成本评分与制度容量上限，绕开内容评估实现监督负载控制，并通过蒙特卡洛分析验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类介入监督在AI高输出速度与高损失场景下存在结构性不可行性。已有研究关注输出速度V，但忽视单位认知负荷L以及能力演化对L各组成部分（分诊、判断、响应）的不对称影响。作者意图提出一种不依赖内容正确性评估的治理范式，打破V×L认知瓶颈。

Method: 将认知负荷分解为分诊成本、判断成本与响应成本，分析AI能力提升对三者的不对称影响。提出Flow-by-Flow范式：基于形式化可计数特征构建认知成本评分函数，对高产量施加非线性成本；搭配制度性容量上限以保持处理量在C_max内。推导四项设计不变量（不判断内容、不可规模化消耗审查能力、身份绑定的逐应用摩擦、无批量清除），并给出参考实现与1,000次参数抽样的蒙特卡洛分析。

Result: 四项设计不变量被证明可共同满足（参考实现展示可行性的同时承认实际困难）。蒙特卡洛分析（1,000次参数绘制）表明复合多指标流量控制方法在90.8%的试验中优于单纯强化监督方案。

Conclusion: 能力提升无法解决监督瓶颈，反而可能使AI通过"遗漏"而非"降低"负荷来扭曲判断成本。Flow-by-Flow提供了一种无需评估内容正确性的治理路径，通过结构性约束而非语义判断来实现可扩展的AI监管，但实际落地仍需解决若干工程与社会挑战。

Abstract: Prior work showed that human-in-the-loop oversight becomes structurally untenable in high-loss domains when AI output velocity V exceeds human cognitive capacity C_max. The operative constraint, however, is not V alone but V x L, where L denotes per-item cognitive load. L consists of triage, judgment, and response, which respond asymmetrically to AI capability improvement. Triage cost does not decline as models become more capable, because semantic indeterminacy is inherent in general-purpose design. Response cost is invariant to accuracy improvements. Only judgment cost faces downward pressure, and this pressure often operates by inducing omission rather than genuine reduction. Capability improvement therefore restructures L rather than reducing it. Governance mechanisms based on evaluating whether AI output is correct either delegate that evaluation to AI and inherit hallucination risk, or delegate it to humans and face the V x L ceiling. We propose Flow-by-Flow, a governance paradigm that controls supervisory load without evaluating content. A cognitive cost score based on formal, countable features imposes nonlinear costs on high-volume production, while an institutional capacity cap keeps processing volume within C_max. We derive four design invariants for any content-judgment-bypass exceedance pathway: no content judgment, no scalable consumption of examiner capacity, identity-bound per-application friction, and no batch clearance. One reference implementation is discussed to show that these invariants are jointly satisfiable, while its practical difficulties are explicitly acknowledged. An illustrative Monte Carlo analysis across 1,000 parameter draws suggests that composite multi-metric flow control outperforms supervision reinforcement alone in 90.8% of trials.

</details>


### [112] [Determinization in Structure Theories: A Unified Framework via Closure, Comparability, and Joint Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07476)
*Hai Hai Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化框架，用于从多元结构理论中构建规范解释（canonical interpretations）。框架区分了三种规范化层级（闭包稳定、全局完备、确定性），将非确定性分类为认知多元性（Type E）与结构多元性（Type S），并提出了两种规范化机制（算子式完备与选择器式构造），同时证明了Type E理论的闭包稳定性与Type S-强理论的确定性，并在LLM辅助推理场景下将幻觉解释为不支持的规范化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样化的结构理论（structure theories）会产生不一致的或非唯一的解释结果，缺乏统一的规范化原则来确保从同一理论出发能收敛到稳定且唯一的解释。现有方法在如何处理非确定性（plurality）以及何种条件下能实现完全确定性方面缺乏系统性的形式化基础，作者旨在建立一个严格的数学框架来刻画和实现规范解释。

Method: 文章提出了一个三元组T=(Σ,A,I)的形式化理论模型，定义了三个规范化层级（closure stabilization、global completion、determinization）。将非确定性分为Type E（认知多元）和Type S（结构多元），并对Type S引入了"强"子类（以共同上界缺失为特征）。提出两种机制：算子式完备化（operator-based completion）与选择器式构造（selector-based construction），给出了这些机制存在的充分结构条件，并通过阶段性算子展示多层级规范化形成结构非交换系统。

Result: 对Type E理论建立了闭包稳定性；对Type S-强理论通过规范选择实现确定性；纯推理式完备化在正的非回归规则加上额外可靠条件下可约化为饱和闭包算子；整体确定性依赖一个尚未解决的全局合流（global confluence）性质；提出了将理论内在机制约化为闭包或选择的条件分类定理。

Conclusion: 该框架为结构理论中的规范解释提供了统一的分类学基础，通过区分多元性类型和规范化层级，阐明了不同条件下可实现的确定性程度。全局合流性质仍为开放问题，且框架在LLM辅助推理等领域有实际应用，将幻觉形式化为不支持的规范化（unsupported canonicalization）。

Abstract: We develop a formal framework for constructing canonical interpretations from plural structure theories. A structure theory is a triple T = (Σ, A, I) consisting of a signature, axioms, and an inference policy, whose admissible interpretation family collects all globally consistent assignments of structural conclusions.
  We distinguish three levels of canonicalization: closure stabilization (per-seed convergence), global completion (seed-independent convergence), and determinization (a unique admissible interpretation). Non-determinism is classified into epistemic plurality (Type E) and structural plurality (Type S), with a refined Type S-strong subclass characterized by the absence of common upper bounds.
  Two canonicalization mechanisms arise: operator-based completion and selector-based construction. We provide sufficient structural conditions under which these mechanisms exist, and show that pure inference-based completion reduces to a saturated closure operator under positive, non-retractive rules with an additional soundness condition. For Type E theories, closure stabilization is established, while full determinization depends on a global confluence property that remains open. For Type S-strong theories, determinization is achieved via canonical selection.
  We further show that multi-level canonicalization forms a structurally non-commutative system via staged operators, and provide a conditional classification theorem reducing theory-intrinsic mechanisms to closure or selection. The framework also applies to LLM-assisted reasoning, where hallucination can be viewed as unsupported canonicalization.

</details>


### [113] [Emotion in an active inference model of human driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07480)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Jens Kober,Martijn Wisse,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的主动推断驾驶模型，将效价（valence）和唤醒（arousal）两个情感维度融入连续状态空间的驾驶场景中，并通过基于当前状态和预测未来结果的联合条件估计来捕捉驾驶过程中的情感信号，在两个交互式驾驶场景中验证了其与真实情感模式的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推断已被广泛用于建模自适应行为，并成功应用于驾驶领域，但现有驾驶模型忽略了情感状态这一影响决策的重要因素。已有的非交通领域工作虽将情感（效价-唤醒模型）引入主动推断，但仅限于离散状态空间的简化场景，无法适用于连续状态的复杂驾驶模型。

Method: 提出一种扩展的效价与唤醒表述，从具有连续状态的复杂主动推断驾驶模型中提取情感估计；关键创新是情感估计不仅基于当前状态，还融合了预测的未来结果，从而更全面地反映驾驶情境中的情感动态。

Result: 在两个交互式驾驶场景中评估了该方法，结果表明所产生的情感信号与类似场景中报告的情感模式相对应，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将情感状态成功纳入连续状态空间的主动推断驾驶模型，填补了该领域的研究空白，为更逼真的驾驶行为建模和具有情感感知能力的自动驾驶系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Active inference has emerged as a principled framework for modeling adaptive behavior by balancing goal-directed action with uncertainty reduction. It has been successfully applied across biological and artificial systems, including recent work on human driving. However, existing active inference models of driving have yet to address an important determinant of behavior in traffic: affective state, which significantly influences decision-making. Prior work in non-traffic domains has explored active inference agents in which emotions are represented along the axes of valence and arousal in the circumplex model. However, this work has been limited to simplified settings with discrete state spaces. In this work, we propose an expanded formulation of valence and arousal that can be extracted from a more complex active inference model of driving with continuous states. In particular, we condition affective estimates not only on the current state but also on predicted future outcomes. We evaluate the proposed approach in two interactive driving scenarios and show that the resulting emotion signals correspond to affective patterns reported in similar scenarios.

</details>


### [114] [Training Variable Long Sequences with Data-Centric Parallel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07524)
*Geng Zhang,Xuanlei Zhao,Kai Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Data-Centric Parallel (DCP)，一种以数据为核心驱动的并行训练方法，通过根据每个batch的序列长度动态调整运行时配置（如并行规模、梯度累积、重计算）来打破长序列训练中效率与易用性之间的权衡，在32块H200 GPU上实现最高2.88倍加速，且仅需10行代码即可集成到任意模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练变长序列的深度学习模型存在显著的计算挑战。现有方法面临效率与易用性之间的艰难权衡：简单方法使用静态配置导致工作负载不均衡、效率低下，而复杂方法则引入大量复杂性和代码改动。作者希望打破这一权衡，找到一种既高效又易于集成的解决方案。

Method: DCP的核心思想是让数据本身驱动运行时。具体而言，它根据每个batch的序列长度动态调整直接运行时设置，包括并行规模（parallel size）、梯度累积（gradient accumulation）和重计算（recomputation）等关键参数，从而消除因序列长度差异带来的工作负载不均衡问题。该方法设计用于泛化，仅需约10行代码即可集成到任何模型中。

Result: 在32块H200 GPU上的实验表明，DCP相比现有方法实现了最高2.88倍的加速，同时保持了极低的集成门槛（10行代码即可适配任意模型）。

Conclusion: 作者认为这种简单而有效的方法有望作为分布式训练领域的一个稳健基线，推动变长序列分布式训练方向的未来发展。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on variable long sequences poses significant computational challenges. Existing methods force a difficult trade-off between efficiency and ease-of-use. Simple approaches use static configurations that cause workload imbalance low efficiency, while complex methods introduces significant complexity and code change for new models. To break this trade-off, we introduce Data-Centric Parallel (DCP). Its core principle is to let the data itself drive the runtime. It achieves this by dynamically adjusting direct runtime settings (e.g., parallel size, gradient accumulation, recomputation) based on each batch's sequence length. Empirical results demonstrate that our method achieves up to a 2.88$\times$ speedup on 32 H200 GPUs. Designed for generalization, it can be integrated into any model with 10 lines of code. We anticipate this simple yet effective approach will serve as a robust baseline and facilitate future advancements in distributed training for variable long sequences.

</details>


### [115] [The Knowing-Saying Gap: When Probes See Errors that Confidence Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07528)
*Jyotin Goel,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 线性探针能以接近完美的准确率检测语言模型中的上下文损坏，但这种检测能力无法转化为可靠的失败预测。跨多跳算术链实验表明，探针在检测损坏与预测最终答案正确性之间存在系统性脱离，且该模式在多个模型家族（包括推理模型）中普遍存在。基于探针的实时监控干预高度依赖模型和错误类型：branch-and-pick策略在各类模型上净收益为正，且在Llama-3.1-8B上唯一不破坏正确轨迹（挽救4个、破坏0个），而reprompt和replace-prior破坏正确轨迹的比率与挽救错误轨迹的比率相当。结论是探针监控是语言化置信度的必要补充，但没有任何单一干预方法占优，可部署的解决方案是模型感知、错误类型感知的路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管线性探针检测上下文损坏的准确率接近完美，但这种高检测精度并未转化为可靠的失败预测能力。研究者希望通过系统探究探针检测能力与实际预测正确性之间的脱离关系，评估探针在部署监控中的实际价值，并为实时监控干预策略提供实证依据。

Method: 在多跳算术链任务上进行受控实验，考察三类探针行为：（1）探测损坏检测与最终答案正确性的关联性；（2）结构化置信度格式下探针的预测行为；（3）探针跨跳数的持久性与结果正确性的关系（预注册的"persistence beats peak"假设检验）。同时测试三种基于探针的干预策略（branch-and-pick、reprompt、replace-prior）在多个模型家族（包括推理模型）上的效果。

Result: 探针检测损坏的准确率接近完美，但对最终答案正确性无预测力；结构化置信度格式下输出坍缩为两个不可区分错误率的值；探针持久性无法区分正确与错误结果，预注册假设被证伪。干预策略上，branch-and-pick在Llama-3.1-8B上净收益为正（4挽救、0破坏），而reprompt和replace-prior的破坏率与挽救率相当。

Conclusion: 探针监控与语言化置信度并非替代关系而是必要互补，但没有任何单一干预策略在所有条件下占优。实际部署应采用模型感知、错误类型感知的路由机制，根据不同模型和错误类型的特性动态选择干预策略。

Abstract: Linear probes detect corrupted context in language models with near-perfect accuracy, yet this does not translate into reliable failure prediction. The result is a dissociation with direct implications for deployment monitoring. Across multi-hop arithmetic chains, probes that detect corruption turn out to be uninformative about final answer correctness; models forced into structured confidence formats collapse to two values with indistinguishable error rates; and probe persistence across hops fails to separate correct from incorrect outcomes, refuting our pre-registered "persistence beats peak" hypothesis. This pattern of knowing but not saying generalises across model families including reasoning models. As a real-time monitor, probe-based interventions are sharply model and error-type dependent: branch-and-pick is net-positive across models and uniquely non-breaking on Llama-3.1-8B (4 rescued, 0 broken), while reprompt and replace-prior break correct traces at roughly the rate they rescue wrong ones. Probe-based monitoring is a necessary complement to verbalised confidence, but no single intervention dominates, and the deployable answer is model-aware, error-type-aware routing.

</details>


### [116] [NL2SHACL-Bench: A Benchmark Suite for Natural Language to SHACL Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07530)
*Yuchen Zhou,Niels Bobet,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NL2SHACL-Bench，这是首个用于自然语言到SHACL翻译评测的基准套件，并基于该基准评估了四个SOTA大语言模型。结果显示当前LLM能够生成语法有效的SHACL，但在复杂逻辑和结构约束的语义等价性上仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编写SHACL形状需要专业技术知识，领域专家难以直接使用。将自然语言需求翻译为SHACL（NL2SHACL）可以降低门槛，但此前缺少专门的基准来评测这一任务，且生成的形状需要超越字符串比较的语义等价性评估方法。

Method: 构建NL2SHACL-Bench基准套件，用于自然语言到SHACL翻译任务的评测。利用该基准评估了四个最先进的大语言模型（LLMs）在NL2SHACL任务上的表现。

Result: 当前LLM在生成语法有效的SHACL方面表现优异，但在为复杂逻辑和结构模式生成语义等价的约束时仍存在困难；NL2SHACL-Bench能够有效衡量这一差距。

Conclusion: NL2SHACL-Bench为NL2SHACL领域提供了有意义且可复用的评测基础，可用于追踪和衡量该方向研究进展。

Abstract: SHACL is a core technology for validating the conformance of RDF knowledge graphs (KGs). Yet, authoring SHACL shapes requires technical expertise that most domain experts lack. Translating natural language requirements into SHACL (NL2SHACL) would lower this barrier. However, there is no dedicated benchmark for NL2SHACL, and evaluating generated shapes requires methods beyond string comparison, as semantically equivalent shapes can differ in serialisation and structure. To tackle these challenges, we present NL2SHACL-Bench, a benchmark suite for natural language to SHACL translation. Using NL2SHACL-Bench, we evaluate four state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) for this task. Our results show that current LLMs are highly capable of generating syntactically valid SHACL, but still struggle to produce semantically equivalent constraints for complex logical and structural patterns. This indicates that NL2SHACL-Bench provides a meaningful basis for measuring advances in the NL2SHACL state of the art.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamic Coalition Formation and Communication Pricing in Skill-Based Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07532)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现代多智能体LLM系统中智能体选择与通信的低效问题，提出将任务建模为合作博弈，通过边际价值激活规则和贪心路由器动态选择智能体与通信边，并以Shapley值预测值得接触的智能体。理论上连接子模最大化，给出曲率优化边界和1/2近似保证；实验表明贪心路由达到暴力搜索最优效用的99.5%，平均仅激活8个中的1.96个智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体AI系统集成了多个异构LLM智能体，但大多数架构要么预先固定通信拓扑，要么允许全广播。这两种方式在token成本、延迟、冗余和错误传播方面都随活动智能体和通信链路数量增加而变得低效，因此需要动态的智能体选择和通信优化机制。

Method: 将智能体选择和通信建模为带任务条件的合作博弈，效用U(C|x)=V(C|x)-Σc_i，区分联盟级成本和智能体激活成本。提出边际价值激活规则和贪心路由器；扩展到带每条边成本的通信边优化；使用估计Shapley值在执行前和过程中预测值得联系的智能体；连接子模最大化，证明曲率精细化边界和双贪心的1/2近似（含符号目标修正），以及Shapley-子模夹心界。

Result: 合成实验中，贪心路由达到暴力搜索最优效用的99.5%，平均激活8个中的1.96个智能体，而全广播仅为38.8%。性能对激活成本和冗余权重鲁棒，但在子模性严重违反或价值估计有噪声时降至66%。

Conclusion: 该框架与Shapley定价、hedonic联盟形成和通信图剪枝等方法有本质区别，为多智能体LLM系统的动态智能体选择和通信优化提供了新范式，建议在真实多智能体LLM基准上进行评估。

Abstract: Modern agentic AI systems combine multiple large language model agents with heterogeneous skills, yet most architectures either fix communication in advance or allow full broadcast. Both can be inefficient because token cost, latency, redundancy, and error propagation increase with the number of active agents and communication links. We model agent selection and communication as a cooperative game with task-conditioned net utility $U(C\mid x)=V(C\mid x)-\sum_{i\in C}c_i$, separating coalition-level costs from agent activation costs. We propose a marginal-value activation rule and greedy router, extend the model to optimize communication edges with per-edge costs, and use estimated Shapley values to predict which agents are worth contacting before and during execution. We connect the problem to submodular maximization and prove two limited guarantees: a curvature-refined bound for a monotone, cardinality-constrained special case, and a tight $1/2$-approximation, with a correction for signed objectives, for an unconstrained non-monotone case via double greedy. Neither guarantee applies directly to the main router, which remains a heuristic. We also prove a Shapley-submodularity sandwich bound linking the error of marginal-value routing to a per-agent diminishing-returns quantity. In synthetic experiments, greedy routing achieves $99.5%$ of brute-force-optimal utility while activating $1.96$ of $8$ agents on average, compared with $38.8%$ for full broadcast. Performance is robust to activation cost and redundancy weight but falls to $66%$ under strong violations of submodularity or noisy value estimates. We distinguish the framework from Shapley pricing, hedonic coalition formation, and communication-graph pruning, and propose evaluation on real multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [118] [MetaSpace: Metamorphic Testing for Spatial Cognition in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07533)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaSpace框架，基于蜕变测试原理评估具身智能体的空间认知能力。通过利用真实执行轨迹中的时空多模态状态，基于逻辑规则和物理定律预定义蜕变关系（MRs），并将其编码为Prolog可执行规则，自动生成测试用例。在三个具身场景中检测到SOTA MLLM驱动智能体共90,422个空间认知错误，所有SOTA智能体得分仅0.44-0.52，远低于人类基准0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体评估存在两大缺陷：一是人工标注VQA对劳动密集且标注质量不稳定；二是高层级任务完成指标（如导航或操控成功率）可能掩盖关键漏洞，使智能体通过次优手段或违反安全的方式完成任务，从而隐藏安全风险与低效问题。由于空间认知是执行具身任务的基石，亟需评估智能体在执行过程中的空间认知鲁棒性。

Method: 借鉴软件工程中的蜕变测试原理，提出MetaSpace框架。核心步骤包括：(1) 从真实执行轨迹中获取时空多模态状态；(2) 基于逻辑规则和物理定律预定义蜕变关系（MRs）；(3) 将MRs编码为Prolog逻辑编程语言中的可执行规则；(4) 自动生成测试用例并检测MRs违反情况，违反即表明空间认知失败。并提出空间认知（SC）分数以量化性能。

Result: 在三个具身场景中的实证评估表明，MetaSpace成功检测到SOTA多模态大语言模型（MLLM）驱动智能体的90,422个空间认知错误。所有SOTA智能体的SC分数平均在0.44-0.52之间，显著低于人类基准0.96。

Conclusion: MetaSpace提供了一种自动化、无需人工标注的具身智能体空间认知评估新范式，能够有效揭示传统任务完成指标所掩盖的认知缺陷。实验结果表明当前SOTA智能体的空间认知能力与人类存在显著差距，凸显了该领域仍需大量改进。

Abstract: An embodied agent is an intelligent entity that interacts with its environment through a physical body. Currently, the evaluation of embodied agents primarily relies on two paradigms: (1) manually annotated Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs and (2) high-level task completion metrics, such as success in navigation or manipulation. The former is labor-intensive and subject to variability in annotation quality. The latter may obscure critical vulnerabilities, allowing agents to complete tasks through suboptimal means or safety violations, thereby concealing safety risks and inefficiencies. Given that spatial cognition is the cornerstone for executing embodied tasks, there is a pressing need to assess whether embodied agents possess robust spatial cognition during task execution.
  Inspired by metamorphic testing principles in software engineering, we propose MetaSpace, a novel framework designed to evaluate the spatial cognition of agents. By leveraging spatiotemporal multimodal states derived from real execution trajectories, MetaSpace automatically generates test cases based on predefined metamorphic relations (MRs) grounded in logical rules and physical laws. Crucially, we encode these MRs as executable rules in a logic programming language (Prolog). Violations of these relations indicate failures in spatial cognition. Our empirical evaluation across three embodied scenarios demonstrates that MetaSpace successfully detects 90,422 spatial cognition errors in state-of-the-art (SOTA) MLLM-driven agents. We introduce the Spatial Cognition (SC) score to quantify performance. Results indicate that all SOTA agents achieve average scores between 0.44 and 0.52, significantly lower than the human benchmark of 0.96.

</details>


### [119] [When LLM Agents Negotiate: Private Information and Dynamic Bargaining in Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07538)
*Chen Liang,Fasheng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM智能体在供应链谈判中的表现，通过对9家模型进行约9840场LLM之间的谈判，以完美贝叶斯均衡为基准进行审计。研究发现：能力决定价值创造与可靠性（谈判延迟侵蚀21-34%的盈余，低阶模型19.2%接受亏本合同）；供应商身份决定盈余分配（模型家族比能力排名更能预测分配，OpenAI约40%、Google约50%、阿里Qwen约70%）；提示词是战略性杠杆，委托人经济耐心与代理人的提示耐心分离是盈余分配最强的驱动因素（占解释方差90%）。论文提出了一个沿效率、分配、可靠性三维度的均衡参考型AI智能体审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体从决策支持向自主采购演进，企业需要知道被委托的谈判智能体是否能创造价值、可预测地分配价值、以及避免亏损合同。以往缺乏在一个规范性的经济学均衡框架下，对LLM谈判行为进行系统化、可解释的审计。

Method: 在一个经典的供应链讨价还价问题中（买方持有私有需求信息，与不知情的卖方谈判数量-支付合同），以验证过的完美贝叶斯均衡（PBE）为基准，对OpenAI、Google、Alibaba的九个LLM进行两两谈判实验，共执行9840场LLM-to-LLM谈判，并系统分析能力水平、供应商身份、提示词设置对谈判结果的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1) 98.9%的谈判达成协议，捕获95.4%的无折现一阶最优盈余，但平均2.98轮vs基准1.25轮，延迟侵蚀21-34%的盈余；2) 基线模型在19.2%的情况下接受个体非理性合同，而中端和旗舰模型仅0.0-0.6%；3) 自博弈买方分享比例：OpenAI约40%、Google约50%、阿里Qwen约70%，供应商身份的预测力优于能力排名；4) 提示词是盈余分配的最强驱动因素，占解释方差的90%。

Conclusion: 论文确立了对AI智能体进行均衡参考型审计的三个维度：折扣效率、分配特征、运营可靠性。结论指出：自动化利益验证是低能力模型下的关键护栏；供应商选择是一阶的分配性决策（可移动7-18个百分点的分配）；提示词中的战略耐心是免费的部署杠杆，可作为委托人控制分配结果的高效手段。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from decision support to autonomous procurement, firms need to know whether delegated negotiators create value, divide it predictably, and avoid money-losing contracts. We study this in a canonical supply chain bargaining problem: a buyer with private demand information negotiates a quantity-payment contract with an uninformed seller. We benchmark nine LLMs from OpenAI, Google, and Alibaba against a validated Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium across 9,840 LLM-to-LLM negotiations. First, capability governs value creation. Agents agree in 98.9% of negotiations and capture 95.4% of first-best surplus undiscounted, but average 2.98 rounds against the benchmark's 1.25, and this delay erodes 21-34% of surplus. Capability also governs reliability: baseline models accept individually irrational contracts in 19.2% of cases, versus 0.0-0.6% at mid-tier and flagship, making automated profit verification the binding guardrail below that threshold. Second, surplus capture is relational. Provider identity predicts who captures surplus better than capability rank: self-play buyer shares average 40% for OpenAI, 50% for Google, and 70% for Alibaba's Qwen, an ordering that survives restricted communication and no discounting. Reversing which provider sells moves the division by 7-18 percentage points, and the capable Qwen flagship is the weakest cross-family seller: vendor choice is a first-order distributional decision. Third, the prompt is a strategic lever. Delegation separates the principal's economic patience from the agent's prompted strategic patience, a free deployment choice that is the single strongest driver of surplus division (90% of explained variance). Together these establish an equilibrium-referenced audit of AI agents along three dimensions: discounted efficiency, distributional profile, and operational reliability.

</details>


### [120] [TREAT: Evaluating Access to Formal Knowledge across Equivalent Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07540)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍TREAT基准测试，用于评估大语言模型能否从等价保真的公式级变换中恢复已知定理身份。该基准包含737个定理身份和29,480个变换样本，最佳模型仅达到60.73%的准确率，揭示了LLM在表示变化下定理知识脆弱性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统需要在灵活输入表示与形式化对象之间运作，但当前挑战在于模型难以识别不熟悉公式化表述所对应的已知形式对象。作者通过定理识别这一具体任务来研究该问题：当定理条件经历等价保真的变换后，模型能否恢复其规范表述下的定理身份。

Method: 从抓取的定理网页中筛选具有可用数学表达式的条目，提取规范定理条件，并通过残差方程、见证语句、优化恒等式、集合关系、算子形式和证明中间表征等六种方式生成等价变换变体，同时记录假设和逆映射。最终构建包含737个定理身份和29,480个变换行的TREAT基准，对多个大语言模型进行测试。

Result: 最佳模型在测试面板上正确检索定理身份的比例仅为60.73%。不同系统展现出多种失败模式，包括弃权（abstention）、错误检测和格式错误输出，表明定理知识在等价的表示变换下可能表现出脆弱性。

Conclusion: TREAT为评估表示鲁棒的形式知识访问提供了一个受控测试平台，其设计理念——稳定的目标对象、明确的等价关系、验证程序和可审计的评分——可推广到需要此类特性的更广泛领域。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly operate between flexible input representations and formal objects used by downstream tools. A key challenge is recognizing when an unfamiliar formulation denotes a known formal object. We study this challenge through theorem recognition: given an equivalence-preserving transformation of a theorem condition, a model must recover the theorem identity associated with the standard statement. We introduce TREAT, a benchmark for evaluating whether large language models can recover known theorem identities from equivalence-preserving formula-level transformations. Rather than paraphrasing theorem text, TREAT changes the mathematical form of theorem conditions themselves, expressing known results through residual equations, witness statements, optimization identities, set relations, operator forms, and proof-intermediate characterizations. Starting from scraped theorem pages, we filter for entries with usable mathematical expression forms, extract canonical theorem conditions, and generate transformed variants with recorded assumptions and inverse mappings. The final corpus contains 737 theorem identities and 29,480 transformed rows. On a test panel, the best model retrieves the correct theorem identity in only 60.73% of cases. Other systems reveal different failure modes, including abstention, wrong detection, and malformed outputs. These suggest that theorem knowledge can be fragile under equivalent changes in representation.
